# HTTP endpoints

## Endpoint: GET /messages/:id/video.mp4

- **Purpose:** Public MP4 bytes as a sized body (`Content-Length` = body byte length) with `Accept-Ranges` / HTTP 206 `Content-Range` so clients can seek. Best-effort faststart (`moov` before `mdat`) on write; heal-on-read remuxes when the stored file is still mdat-first. `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *`. Public: hidden rows (`deletedAt` set) are still 404 even when the on-disk file remains. A live reply without an account whose author pubkey is not a recorded zapper (or that has no author pubkey) is the same 404, matching `GET /messages/:id`. Founder/moderator Bearer (`roleAtLeast(..., 'moderator')`): serve hidden-row bytes (keep Range / `Content-Length` / CORS) with `Cache-Control: private, no-store` and `Vary: Authorization` so a staff GET cannot refill a public CDN cache. After deploy, purge or wait out CDN cache for URLs previously served without `Content-Length` (chunked streams that ignored `Range`).
- **Errors:** 404 `{ error: 'Video not found' }` (missing / wrong ext / empty / live withheld zapper reply / soft-hidden without a founder/moderator Bearer); 416 unsatisfiable `Range` (`Content-Range: bytes */SIZE`); 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }`.
- **Used by:** Damus/Primal/Safari kind:1 video URLs.
- **Auth:** none for live public GET; founder/moderator Bearer for a hidden row.

## Endpoint: GET /messages/:id/video.webm

- **Purpose:** Same as `video.mp4` for WebM posts (sized body + Range; WebM is not remuxed). Public: hidden still 404. Staff Bearer: serve hidden-row bytes (keep Range / `Content-Length` / CORS) with `Cache-Control: private, no-store` and `Vary: Authorization`.
- **Errors:** Same 404 / 416 / 503.
- **Used by:** Damus/Primal/Safari.
- **Auth:** none for live public GET; founder/moderator Bearer for a hidden row.

## Endpoint: GET /messages/:id/video.mov

- **Purpose:** Same as `video.mp4` for QuickTime posts (sized body + Range + faststart). Public: hidden still 404. Staff Bearer: serve hidden-row bytes (keep Range / `Content-Length` / CORS) with `Cache-Control: private, no-store` and `Vary: Authorization`.
- **Errors:** Same 404 / 416 / 503.
- **Used by:** Damus/Primal/Safari.
- **Auth:** none for live public GET; founder/moderator Bearer for a hidden row.

## Endpoint: DELETE /messages/:id

- **Purpose:** Bearer required. A moderator soft-hides a forum note: stamps `deleted_at` / `deleted_by` on the target and every untagged **direct** reply via `MessageStore.markDeleted`. Does not hard-delete rows, media, invoices, zap receipts, or gifts; does not call `deleteById`. Already-tagged targets keep original stamps and still return 204. When the target itself is external (`accountId === null && authorPubkey !== null`), after `markDeleted` succeeds the route calls the single atomic `blockPubkeyAndHideRows` operation to add the staff kill-switch entitlement block and soft-hide every other live row from that pubkey, then logs `messages.external.blocked` with `{ messageId, hidden: cascaded + 1 }`. Hiding a member note that merely has external children does not block a pubkey or hide that pubkey's other rows; only the normal direct-reply cascade applies. After `markDeleted` succeeds, every affected shop identity has its durable place outbox queued; the sender decides DELETE from current state, and delivery failures do not change the 204. This holds for a keyed top-level shop whatever its pin was when the route read it, so a pin set concurrently does not stay on the map; a shop note that never had a key was never sent and sends nothing. Live public JSON never exposes the stamps; staff GET of a hidden row may include them. After a successful stamp (including already tagged), best-effort NIP-09 `kind: 5` is published for the target and each direct child with a non-empty `eventId` and non-null `accountId`, signed with that row's custodial nsec (not the staff deleter), to the durability relay plus the public relay list (not gated on `NOSTR_PUBLISH` / `NOSTR_PUBLISH_PUBLIC`). Then, when `CLOUDFLARE_ZONE_ID` and `CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN` are set and `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` resolves, public photo/video URLs for those rows are purged at Cloudflare (`purge_cache` files, chunks of 30). Sign/publish/purge failure still 204; logs `messages.delete.nostr_failed` / `messages.delete.purge_failed` / `messages.delete.retract_failed` with `messageId` only (never nsec, token, or post text). After the stamp (including already tagged), also best-effort retract in-app notifications whose `parentId` or `replyId` is the note or a direct child (`listChildIds` then `deleteByMessageIds([id, ...childIds])`). Retract / `listChildIds` failure logs `messages.delete.notifications_failed` and still 204 (never 503). Debug restore does not undelete Nostr or un-purge. Logs `messages.deleted` with `messageId`, `accountId`, and staff `role` (never post text).
- **Open credit:** A credit note (`goalRepayable === true`) that has received sats cannot be hidden by any role, founder included, until every share of its repayment plan is paid. A credit that never filled, has no term, has no payable plan, whose debt cannot be priced, or that holds sats not yet assigned to a giver account (`sumUnassignedCreditSats > 0`) stays open; a later assignment can therefore never reopen a credit that was already hidden. The check reads `sumUnassignedCreditSats` first: any unassigned sats keep the credit open on their own. Only then does it read `listCreditPayers` and `listRepayments` and build the schedule from the assigned givers with `payerDebtUnits` and `repaymentSchedule`; `GET /messages/:id/repayment` additionally weighs unassigned sats, which the check never needs because it already stopped. Reading unassigned sats first means a receipt assigned to a giver in between is never missed. The check runs only on a live note: an already-tagged credit keeps its stamps and returns 204 like any other tagged target. A live credit is hidden with `markDeleted(…, expectedSats)`, a compare-and-swap on `sats`: when a payment lands between the check and the hide, nothing is stamped and the route answers 409; when another hide lands first, 204; when the row is gone, 404. A refusal logs `messages.delete.open_credit` with `messageId` and `accountId` and writes nothing. Replies under a credit are not affected.
- **Errors:** 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` without a session; 403 `{ error: 'Forbidden' }` when the live role is not at least moderator (including the author); 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for a non-UUID `:id` or missing row; 409 `{ error: 'An open credit cannot be deleted' }` for a live open credit note; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` when the store throws (`messages.delete.failed`). NIP-09 / purge / notification retract failure is never 503. 403 `{ error: 'SUNDAY_REST' }` when `Time-Zone` names an IANA zone that is Sunday on the server clock (a missing, blank, or invalid zone does not refuse).
- **Used by:** Staff hide controls in the app forum.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session (moderator).

## Endpoint: PATCH /messages/:id/place

- **Purpose:** Bearer required. A moderator sets or replaces the map pin on a live top-level shop note (`#21GiftsShop`) via `MessageStore.setPlace`. A non-null shop pin must have a short name that is not the address and a full structured address (`shopPlaceProblem`); otherwise 400 and nothing is stored. After that, the stored description must pass `shopTextProblem` against the new pin (20–1000 characters and longer than the shop name); otherwise 400 and nothing is stored. A listed-shop write creates its identity when needed and atomically queues the durable OpenCryptoPay place outbox. The same write queues the photo outbox when the changed note is a public shop. `place: null` is 400 `Shop name is required` and nothing is stored, so this route does not clear a shop name. The sender reads the note when it sends (`listedShop`): a listed shop gets PUT, a public shop that is not map-complete (`publicShop` set and `listedShop` null) clears the place flag and sends nothing, and a keyed shop that is no longer public (tag removed, till account cleared, hidden, or gone) gets DELETE, so an older complete version does not stay on the map when the shop itself is gone; each send carries a new revision, so concurrent writes reach the map in order and the last send matches the last write. This route does not accept `place: null`, so it does not queue a clear. A concurrent store write that drops the pin while the note stays public sends nothing. A hide still makes the next send DELETE. A later complete pin before the send is one PUT. Does not republish Nostr, change note text, or notify. A missing `place` key is 400 `Invalid body`. A real change appends `message_edit` with the full place JSON including `address`. An identical pin does not. Success is the live public message JSON (optional `place` with `address`, reply count, no hide stamps).
- **Errors:** 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` without a session; 403 `{ error: 'Forbidden' }` when the live role is not at least moderator; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for a non-UUID `:id`, missing or hidden row, or `setPlace` false; 400 `{ error: 'Invalid body' }` for non-JSON or a missing `place` key; 400 `{ error: 'Place must be a latitude and longitude' }`; 400 `{ error: 'Place label must be at most 80 characters' }`; 400 `{ error: 'Place address is invalid' }`; 400 `{ error: 'Shop name is required' }` when the label is empty or `place` is null; 400 `{ error: 'Shop address is required' }`; 400 `{ error: 'Shop name must not contain the address' }`; 400 `{ error: 'Shop description must be 20 to 1000 characters and longer than the shop name' }` when a non-null pin passes `shopPlaceProblem` and the stored description fails `shopTextProblem`; 400 `{ error: 'A reply cannot include a place' }`; 400 `{ error: 'Only a shop note can set a place' }`; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }`. 403 `{ error: 'SUNDAY_REST' }` when `Time-Zone` names an IANA zone that is Sunday on the server clock (a missing, blank, or invalid zone does not refuse).
- **Used by:** Staff shop map pin in the app forum.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session (moderator).

## Endpoint: PATCH /messages/:id/shop-account

- **Purpose:** Bearer required. A moderator sets or replaces the 21.gifts account on a live top-level shop note (`#21GiftsShop`) via `MessageStore.setShopAccount`. The assignment is not the note author. Does not republish Nostr or change note text. `username: null` is 400 `A shop note needs a shop account` once the row is a live top-level shop note, and nothing is stored; a missing `username` key is 400 `Invalid body`. A real change appends `message_edit`. An unchanged account does not. The assignment is the account id: a new name or username on that same id is not a change. After the write, a note that is now a public shop gets its identity when needed and the durable place outbox is queued. The same write queues the photo outbox when the changed note is a public shop. The sender reads the note when it sends (`listedShop`): a listed shop gets PUT, a public shop that is not map-complete clears the place flag and sends nothing, and a keyed shop that is no longer public (tag removed, till account cleared, hidden, or gone) gets DELETE, so an older complete version does not stay on the map when the shop itself is gone; each send carries a new revision, so concurrent writes reach the map in order and the last send matches the last write. Delivery failures do not change the response. Success is the live public message JSON including `shopAccount` `{ id, username, name }` (reply count, no hide stamps).
- **Errors:** 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` without a session; 403 `{ error: 'Forbidden' }` when the live role is not at least moderator; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for a non-UUID `:id`; a missing or hidden row is 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` before the handle is searched; `setShopAccount` false is 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }`; 400 `{ error: 'Invalid body' }` when the body is not a JSON object or has no `username` key; 400 `{ error: 'Username is not valid' }`; 404 `{ error: 'No account with that username' }` only after the row is a live shop note; 400 `{ error: 'A reply cannot include a shop account' }`; 400 `{ error: 'Only a shop note can set a shop account' }`; 400 `{ error: 'A shop note needs a shop account' }` when `username` is null on a live top-level shop note; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }`. 403 `{ error: 'SUNDAY_REST' }` when `Time-Zone` names an IANA zone that is Sunday on the server clock (a missing, blank, or invalid zone does not refuse).
- **Used by:** The app shops feed.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session (moderator).

## Endpoint: PATCH /messages/:id/text

- **Purpose:** Bearer required. A moderator changes the text of a live top-level shop note (`#21GiftsShop`) via `MessageStore.updateText`. The shop tag is kept or restored. When the note already has a complete pin (`shopPlaceProblem` is null), the new description must pass `shopTextProblem` (20–1000 characters and longer than the shop name) or the response is 400 and nothing is stored. With no pin or an incomplete legacy pin, the description is not checked against it. Name and address are not re-checked here. After a real write, a note that is now a public shop gets its identity when needed and the durable place outbox is queued; the sender decides PUT or DELETE from current state. The same write queues the photo outbox when the changed note is a public shop. The sender reads the note when it sends (`listedShop`): a listed shop gets PUT, and a public shop that is not map-complete (`publicShop` set and `listedShop` null) clears the place flag and sends nothing, and a keyed shop that is no longer public (tag removed, till account cleared, hidden, or gone) gets DELETE, so an older complete version does not stay on the map; each send carries a new revision, so concurrent writes reach the map in order and the last send matches the last write. A keyed shop whose stored pin lost its address (a concurrent place edit) stays public and sends no place request after a text write. The photo outbox stays queued, and a non-empty photo set can be sent without a complete address. A shop whose pin never had an address still gets a key when it is public, and sends no place request. Does not republish Nostr, notify, or change sats, media, author, mentions, event ids, or publish state. An unchanged body is 200 without a history row or a new outbox generation, except when the note has a complete pin and the description fails `shopTextProblem`: that is 400 before the short-circuit and nothing is stored. A real change appends `message_edit`. Success is the live public message JSON (reply count, no hide stamps, no `edits` field).
- **Errors:** In order: 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` without a session; 403 `{ error: 'Forbidden' }` when the live role is not at least moderator; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for a non-UUID `:id`; 400 `{ error: 'Invalid body' }` when the body is not a JSON object or `text` is missing or not a string; 400 `{ error: 'Text must be 1–8000 characters' }` before the row is read; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for a missing row, a hidden row, or `updateText` undefined; 400 `{ error: 'A reply cannot be edited' }`; 400 `{ error: 'Only a shop note can be edited' }`; 400 `{ error: 'Text must be 1–8000 characters or include a photo' }` when the normalized text is empty and the note has neither a photo nor a video; the shop tag is then kept or restored, and if that exceeds 8000 characters, 400 `{ error: 'Text must be 1–8000 characters' }`; 400 `{ error: 'Shop description must be 20 to 1000 characters and longer than the shop name' }` when the note has a complete pin and the stripped description fails `shopTextProblem`; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }`. 403 `{ error: 'SUNDAY_REST' }` when `Time-Zone` names an IANA zone that is Sunday on the server clock (a missing, blank, or invalid zone does not refuse).
- **Used by:** Staff shop-note text edit in the app forum.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session (moderator).

## Endpoint: PATCH /messages/:id/photos

- **Purpose:** Bearer required. A moderator replaces the stills on a live top-level shop note (`#21GiftsShop`) via `MessageStore.replacePhotos`. Body `{ photos: { contentType, data, takenAt? }[] }` (at most 10; empty clears stills). Does not republish Nostr, write `message_edit`, or change text, video, sats, author, or event ids. A video note keeps its video. A public shop queues the durable photo outbox (live, top-level, tagged `#21GiftsShop`, with a till account; a pin is not required). On Postgres, when a `shop_identity` row exists, `trg_shop_identity_cover` and `trg_shop_identity_extra_photo` queue it in that write when `DIRECTORY_SQL` matches the note. The memory adapter does not queue it in this request: `replacePhotos` only raises the photo revision, and `ocpPhotosPending` is set on the next `#sync`, which this route does not call. Success is the live public message JSON.
- **Errors:** In order: 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }`; 403 `{ error: 'Forbidden' }` below moderator; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for a non-UUID `:id`; 400 `{ error: 'Invalid body' }`; 400 `{ error: 'At most 10 photos' }`; 400 `{ error: 'Photo must be a JPEG, PNG, or WebP under 1 MiB' }` before the row is read, including when that row is missing or hidden; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for a missing row, a hidden row, or `replacePhotos` undefined; 400 `{ error: 'A reply cannot be edited' }`; 400 `{ error: 'Only a shop note can be edited' }`; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }`. 403 `{ error: 'SUNDAY_REST' }` when `Time-Zone` names an IANA zone that is Sunday on the server clock (a missing, blank, or invalid zone does not refuse).
- **Used by:** The shop-note pencil wizard in the app.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session (moderator).

## Endpoint: GET /messages/:id/edits

- **Purpose:** Bearer required. A moderator lists `message_edit` history for a top-level shop note, newest first, including a hidden shop note. Does not change the note and does not republish Nostr. GET is not a Sunday write. Success is `{ edits: [{ id, createdAt, field, before, after, actor }] }`, newest first. `createdAt` is ISO. `field` is `text`, `place`, or `shopAccount` (SQL `shop_account` is published as `shopAccount`). `before` and `after` are the previous and next value: a text string, a place pin or null, or a shop account `{ id, username, name }` or null. `before.username` is null when the previous till account had no username; `after` always carries a username, since `PATCH /messages/:id/shop-account` refuses an account without one. `actor` is `{ id, name, role }`. A missing account keeps `{ id, name: null, role: null }`. Public message JSON does not include `edits`. Empty history is `{ edits: [] }`.
- **Errors:** 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` without a session; 403 `{ error: 'Forbidden' }` when the live role is not at least moderator; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for a non-UUID `:id`, a missing row, a reply, or a note that is not a shop note; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }`. GET history is not refused with `SUNDAY_REST`. An operator `DEBUG_TOKEN` or `DEBUG_READ_TOKEN` skips these 401/403 answers (no session, no role check).
- **Used by:** Staff shop-note history in the app forum.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session (moderator). The operator `DEBUG_TOKEN` or `DEBUG_READ_TOKEN` reads the same body without a session (`isOperatorBearer`); a bearer that matches neither falls through to this session check.

## Endpoint: GET /messages/hidden

- **Purpose:** Bearer session required (moderator), or the operator token. Inverse **read** of `DELETE /messages/:id`. Lists soft-hidden forum rows (`deletedAt` set) newest-hidden first (`deletedAt` desc, then `id` desc), capped at 200, via `listHidden` / `serializeHiddenMessage`. Listed rows include every hidden row regardless of external-zapper entitlement — this endpoint is a moderation view and is intentionally unaffected by the read-visibility rule described above. Each item includes stored `name` (no empty-name pubkey fallback), ISO `createdAt` / `deletedAt`, `hasPhoto` / `photoCount` / `photoTakenAts` (length equals `photoCount`; null when unknown; `[]` when there are no stills; `photoTakenAt` only when `photoCount === 1`) / `hasVideo` / `videoContentType`, optional `goalSats` (positive integer on a top-level note; omitted when unset/null/0/absent or on a reply), optional `goalRepayable: true` only when stored true (omitted when null, never false, omitted on a reply), optional `goalTermDays` when stored (omitted when null, omitted on a reply), `goalCurrency`, `goalAmount`, and the four `goalAmount*` snapshots when `goalCurrency` is stored (omitted on a legacy row; a snapshot may be null), optional `place` when a pin is stored (omitted when unset), optional `shopAccount` (`{ id, username, name }`) when a shop account is stored (omitted when unset or when the till account has no username), always-present `parentId` (JSON `null` on top-level), and `deletedBy: { id, name, role }` resolved from `authStore.getAccount` (missing account keeps the id with `name` / `role` null; null `deletedBy` is `{ id: null, name: null, role: null }`). Optional `via: 'nostr'` is present exactly when `accountId === null && authorPubkey !== null`, the same rule as public message JSON; the pubkey itself is never included. Includes `accountId` when the author is a 21.gifts account and omits it for an external row. Never includes `eventId`, `nostrPublishState`, `payable`, `authorCanReceive`, author `role`, `nostrEvent`, `claimedUntil`, `contentFp`, nsec, or photo/video bytes. Unsigned/non-staff list/GET/photo/video stay 404 for hidden rows; a founder/moderator session may GET the hidden permalink and photo/video. No `forum.read` gate — a moderator without rules agreement is still 200. Logs `messages.hidden.listed` with `{ count }` only (never post text, never message ids). Registered before public `GET /messages/:id` so `"hidden"` is not captured as `:id`. No staff UNHIDE session route.
- **Errors:** 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` without a session; 403 `{ error: 'Forbidden' }` when the live role is not at least moderator (including the author / verified); 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` when the store, deleter lookup, or serialize throws (`messages.hidden.list_failed`). An operator `DEBUG_TOKEN` or `DEBUG_READ_TOKEN` skips these 401/403 answers (no session, no role check).
- **Used by:** Staff hidden-note log in the app forum.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session (moderator). The operator `DEBUG_TOKEN` or `DEBUG_READ_TOKEN` reads the same body without a session (`isOperatorBearer`); a bearer that matches neither falls through to this session check.

## Endpoint: GET /.well-known/nostr.json

- **Purpose:** NIP-05 directory `{ names, relays }`. Account locals are allocated first (stored username wins over display-name slug; nameless accounts with a stored username are included), then currently public shops in stable shop-key creation order. Both allocations use one read of the accounts per directory computation, so a shop never takes an account's local. The directory is the shared cache of `nip05Directory` (60 s per store set, the same names as `GET /shops/:npub` and the shop kind 0), so the full body may be up to 60 s old, matching its `max-age=60`. CORS `*`. Optional `?name=` filters either kind; a name the cached directory does not hold, or one it gives a shop while an account now holds it as its username, forces one fresh directory first (at most one computation per 10 s per store set). A `?name=` is answered with lookups by local part in the directory's maps (`accountsByLocal`, `shopsByLocal`), never a walk over every account or shop.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Directory is unavailable' }`.
- **Used by:** Damus verification; app proxies this from the site apex.
- **Auth:** none.

## Endpoint: GET /.well-known/lnurlp/:username

- **Purpose:** LUD-16 payRequest for `<username>@<host of PUBLIC_BASE_URL>` (in production `username@21.gifts`). A member receives only on their in-app wallet: when `LNURL_SERVER_URL` and `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` resolve and the account has a verified wallet key (`sparkPubkeyVerifiedAt` is a number), the pay request comes from the self-hosted LNURL server (`GET /.well-known/lnurlp/<username>`, 5 s timeout, fixed `Host`, 120 requests per minute per client address); it is returned only when `walletPayRequest` accepts it with callback `<PUBLIC_BASE_URL>/lnurlp/<username>/invoice`. CORS `*`. `Cache-Control: no-store` so a public cache cannot keep the pin or the unpinned range. There is no pass-through to any other provider. After that resolve, `ShopPayments.payLink` decides the answer: an open shop payment (status `open`, window not ended) answers the OpenCryptoPay pay request (`shopPayRequest`: callback `<PUBLIC_BASE_URL>/lnurlp/cb/<username>`, both bounds the payment's millisats, `standard` `OpenCryptoPay`, `quote { id, expiration, payment }`, `requestedAmount` in BTC as a plain JSON number, the current quote's `transferAmounts`); the current quote is replaced (at most 4 times, not in the window's last 45 s) when less than 45 s remain, and an expired one gets a Lightning-only quote. A pending charge with no shop payment first starts or joins the creation procedure and waits for it at most until 8 s after the fetch arrived (`SHOP_PAY_LINK_CREATION_WAIT_MS`), with or without `timeout`, whatever the creation is still waiting for, lock waits included: a charge has at most one creation in flight from when it is queued until it ends, shared by every request for that charge and by the creation `POST /pos` starts; the arrival is taken as the route's first statement, so the wallet pay request and a creation still queued behind another procedure of the account (a payer creation, another charge's creation, a quote replacement, a holder that does not finish) count against the 8 s, a creation that outlasts the wait goes on in the background, and a creation that ends earlier (re-reads and stops, or fails) ends the wait right away. It then reads the account's state again whatever the creation's outcome (a payment, skipped, failed, or the wait used up) and answers from it. A request with `timeout` that reaches this point 8 s or more after it arrived still starts or joins the creation but does not wait, and answers today's pinned pay request. When the open payment leaves `open` while its quote is being read or replaced, the state is also read again: another payment that is the open amount now is answered as the open amount, once per request; otherwise, or when that one also left `open`, a charge still waiting keeps its pin. Otherwise today's pay request is returned, with both sendable bounds pinned to the charge amount while the pending charge is waiting (no shop payment, or an `ended` one; a charge with an `open` payment is the open amount), and unpinned when its shop payment is `accepted` or `paid`. With `timeout` (digits only, capped at 10 s, else 0) the api re-reads every 250 ms, at most 50 waiting requests per process, until an open amount or a pending charge whose shop payment is missing or `open` appears; with neither it answers 404 with the OpenCryptoPay body (`noPendingPaymentBody`). With `method` and an open amount it answers as `GET /lnurlp/cb/:username` for the current quote (simplified flow); when that payment leaves `open` after its quote was read and before the callback reads the state, the state is read again as above (another open amount once per request, otherwise that 404, never `Quote expired`); with `method` and no open amount it is that 404. Usernames `invoice`, `cb`, and any ending in `.` never get stablecoin rows and keep today's pay request (pinned while a till charge is pending), also with `timeout`, `method`, or `asset`: no wait and no 404.
- **Errors:** Every error body except the no-pending-payment 404 is `shopErrorBody`: `{ statusCode, message, error, status: 'ERROR', reason }` with the text in `message`, `error`, and `reason`. 404 `Not found` when the username is invalid or unknown, the account has no verified wallet, or the LNURL server is not configured; an upstream 404 returns the same 404; over the limit returns 429 `Too many requests`; an unreachable LNURL server, another non-2xx status, unparsable JSON, or a document `walletPayRequest` rejects returns 503 `Lightning Address could not be resolved`; 502 `Lightning Address could not be resolved` when the store throws; 404 `No pending payment found` for `timeout` with nothing waiting or `method` without an open amount (never for the usernames `invoice`, `cb`, or ending in `.`); its body is only `noPendingPaymentBody`, the standard's example: `id`, `displayName`, `standard`, `possibleStandards`, `displayQr`, `recipient`, plus `statusCode` 404, `message` and `reason` `No pending payment found`, `error` `Not Found` (the HTTP reason phrase, as in that example), and `status` `ERROR`.
- **Used by:** Lightning wallets paying `<username>@<host of PUBLIC_BASE_URL>`; OpenCryptoPay wallets (Tether Wallet) scanning the shop sticker; the app's payment page, which proxies this from the site apex.
- **Auth:** none.

## Endpoint: GET /mentions

- **Purpose:** Signed-in suggestions for `@` in a forum post or reply. Query `q` is optional. Omitted, empty, whitespace, or a lone `@` after trim is the first 20 handles in alphabetical order. Otherwise the value is trimmed, one leading `@` is stripped, and the rest is lowercased. That remainder must match `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]{0,31}$` or the route returns 400. A valid token matches the start of the username, the start of a username segment split on `.` `_` `-`, the start of the display name, or a display-name word split on space `.` `_` `-`. A token that itself contains `.`, `_`, or `-` matches only a whole-string start. Each row is `{ id, username, name }` where `name` is the trimmed display name, or the stored username when that name is blank. At most 20 rows, ordered by `lower(trim(username))` then `id`. Blank usernames are skipped. Does not parse or store `@username` marks on a note.
- **Errors:** 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` without a usable bearer session; 409 `{ error: 'missing_requirements', missing }` when `forum.read` is not allowed yet; 400 `{ error: 'Invalid query' }` when the remainder after trim and one leading `@` is not a username prefix.
- **Used by:** The forum composer suggestion list (`GET /forum/mentions` on the app, which proxies here).
- **Auth:** Bearer session with `forum.read`.

## Endpoint: GET /accounts/by-username/:username

- **Purpose:** Exact lookup of one account by username for a signed-in forum reader. `:username` is trimmed and lowercased and must be a LUD-16 local-part (`normalizeUsername`); otherwise 400. The match is `lower(trim)` of the stored username. The response `username` is that stored string, not the normalised param. `name` is the stored display name, including `null`, and is not replaced by the username when blank. The body is exactly `{ id, username, name }`. A missing account, or a stored username that is not a non-empty string, is 404. Does not list suggestions.
- **Errors:** 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` without a usable bearer session; 409 `{ error: 'missing_requirements', missing }` when `forum.read` is not allowed yet; 400 `{ error: 'Username is not valid' }` when `normalizeUsername` returns null; 404 `{ error: 'No account with that username' }` when no account matches or the stored username is blank.
- **Used by:** A client that already has the exact handle and needs that account's id and display name.
- **Auth:** Bearer session with `forum.read`.

## Endpoint: GET /pay/:username

- **Purpose:** Public, unauthenticated pay-link card for a member. Normalises `:username`, loads the account, and returns `name`, `username`, `minSats`, `maxSats`, and `charge` from the LNURL-pay metadata of the member's receiving address (`receivingAddress`: their verified wallet, resolved internally against the LNURL server). `name` is the trimmed display name, or the normalised username when the display name is blank. No charge → `charge: null` and the wallet sat range. Unexpired pending → both bounds equal that amount and `charge` is `{ id, amountSats, expiresAt }` plus `amountCurrency`, `amount`, `amountUsd`, `amountChf`, `amountEur`, and `amountPhp`. A sats-only charge carries those six fields as null. A bad wallet window is still 502 before any pin. Does not return the callback, the Lightning Address, or provider metadata. No spend token.
- **Errors:** 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` when the username is invalid, the account is unknown, or it has no receiving address (no verified wallet, or the LNURL server is not configured); 502 `{ error: 'Lightning Address could not be resolved' }` when the LNURL server is unreachable, the store throws, `currentPending` throws, `minSendable` or `maxSendable` is not a safe integer, or `maxSats < minSats`.
- **Used by:** A browser pay page that uses GET /pay/:username to show an open till instead of a free amount.
- **Auth:** none.

## Endpoint: GET /pay/charges/:id

- **Purpose:** Public till receipt. `serializePosCharge`: `id`, `amountSats`, `status`, `createdAt`, `expiresAt`, `paidAt`, and the six fiat fields. `accountId` and `sparkInvoice` are omitted. A paid charge is returned. Registered before `/:username`.
- **Errors:** 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` when the id is missing.
- **Used by:** A browser pay page opening a receipt by charge id.
- **Auth:** none.

## Endpoint: GET /pay/:username/charges/:id

- **Purpose:** Same receipt when the charge belongs to the normalised `:username`. `serializePosCharge` and nothing secret. A paid charge is returned. Registered before `/:username`.
- **Errors:** 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` when the username is invalid or unknown, the charge is missing, or another member owns it.
- **Used by:** A browser pay page opening a receipt on that member's pay link.
- **Auth:** none.

## Endpoint: POST /pay/:username/invoice

- **Purpose:** Public, unauthenticated BOLT11 mint for an exact satoshi amount. Same account lookup as `GET /pay/:username`, then `{ amountSats: number, comment?: string }`: `amountSats` must be an integer inside `[minSats, maxSats]` whose millisatoshi value sits inside the provider window. A different POST amount while a charge is open is the existing 400 and does not call the invoice callback. The charge amount must still sit in the provider millisatoshi window or that same 400 is returned and the callback is not called. Settlement calls `requestGiftInvoice` on the member's receiving address, the verified in-app wallet resolved internally through the LNURL server; there is no fallback to an external address. Response is `{ pr, amountSats, sparkInvoice }`; `comment` is never sent to the invoice callback. No spend token. While a point-of-sale charge is pending, the BOLT11's payment hash is recorded against it (`recordInvoice`, one payment hash claim transaction) so the paid watcher can see it settle; when another charge's invoice row or another shop payment already holds that hash (`other_payment`), the BOLT11 is not handed out (see Errors). With free in-app payments on, `sparkInvoice` is non-null. While a charge is pending (the till pin makes the amount the charge amount) and `comment` is ignored, it is a `spark1…` invoice for exactly that amount to the shop's verified wallet key, memo `pos:<chargeId>`, issued once per charge and returned again on later calls. Without a pending charge it is a `spark1…` invoice for exactly `amountSats` to the member's verified wallet key, minted after `pr`, not stored and not watched (no zap receipt, no gift record); its memo is the trimmed `comment` cut to the member's `commentAllowed` characters (missing is 0) and then to 120 UTF-8 bytes without splitting a character, and a blank, non-string, or not-allowed comment leaves the memo out. A 21.gifts in-app wallet pays either without a fee. With free in-app payments off `sparkInvoice` is `null`; a store failure on either step is logged and the response is still `{ pr, amountSats, sparkInvoice: null }` or carries the Spark invoice already issued.
- **Errors:** 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for an invalid username, unknown account, or no receiving address (no verified wallet, or the LNURL server is not configured); 400 `{ error: 'Enter a whole number of sats' }` for missing or invalid JSON, a non-integer, or an amount outside the window; 502 `{ error: 'Lightning Address could not be resolved' }` when the address cannot be resolved, the store throws, `currentPending` throws, the sat window is empty or not a safe integer, the invoice fetch fails, or the BOLT11 is missing, not a safe integer amount, or not for that exact millisatoshi amount; the same 502 when `recordInvoice` returns `other_payment` for the pending charge (logs `shop.alert.duplicate_invoice` with `chargeId`, `holderChargeId`, and `paymentId` only when a shop payment holds the hash; no Spark invoice is issued). A `recordInvoice` error logs `pos.invoice.record_failed` and the BOLT11 is still handed out.
- **Used by:** The browser pay page minting the open till amount when a charge is present, not a freely typed amount; the in-app wallet's Send to a member on this host, which pays `sparkInvoice` fee-free and falls back to Lightning when it is `null`.
- **Auth:** none.

## Endpoint: POST /pay/:username/payment

- **Purpose:** Public, unauthenticated opening of a shop payment for an amount the payer entered on the payment page (`docs/shop-stablecoin-payment.md`). Same account lookup as `GET /pay/:username`, then body `{ amountSats: number }` (an integer of at least 1; `comment` is ignored). Runs `ShopPayments.createForPayer`: under the account's in-process creation lock it re-checks for a pending charge, applies the 60 s no-pair wait and the two creation counters (60 per minute for the api, 10 per minute per account, per process), reads Orchestra's route list (a failed fetch stops without the no-pair wait; no candidate pair starts it), mints one BOLT11 with one 4 s deadline, requests one exact-out Orchestra quote per candidate pair within 3 s, keeps the quotes that pass `checkOffer`, and inserts the payment, its first quote, and its offers in one transaction that takes the payment hash claim, then the account row lock. After the locks that transaction writes nothing when the request arrived 16 s ago or longer (the arrival is taken as the handler's first statement) or the client disconnected (the request's abort signal). An `open` payer-opened payment of the account ends with `replaced` (`window` when its window passed); when the BOLT11's payment hash is already held by another shop payment or recorded against a charge, the insert transaction stops before anything is written, so that `open` payment stays `open`. 200 `{ payment, expiresAt }` where `expiresAt` is the window end, five minutes after creation. The route group is always mounted; a payment opens only when `LNURL_SERVER_URL` and `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` resolve.
- **Errors:** In this order: 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for an invalid username, unknown account, or no receiving address (no verified wallet, or the LNURL server is not configured); 502 `{ error: 'Lightning Address could not be resolved' }` when the wallet's pay request cannot be resolved or the store throws (also a store throw during the creation, which logs `pay.failed`; the page then repeats the request); 400 `{ error: 'Enter a whole number of sats' }` for missing or invalid JSON or an amount that is not an integer of at least 1; 409 `{ error: 'A payment is already open', code: 'payment_open' }` while a till charge is pending (checked again under the lock and in the insert transaction); 400 `{ error: 'Enter a whole number of sats' }` for an amount outside the pay request's range; 503 `{ error: 'Stablecoin payment is unavailable', code: 'stablecoin_unavailable' }` for the usernames `invoice`, `cb`, or ending in `.`, when `resolveShopPaymentConfig` is off, during the no-pair wait, or over a creation counter; 503 `stablecoin_unavailable` when the route list cannot be read (also when the route-list budget of 25 per minute is used up) or no pair is a candidate (both before the mint); 502 `{ error: 'Lightning Address could not be resolved' }` when the mint fails, the BOLT11 is for another amount, or its payment hash is already held by another shop payment or recorded against a charge (`findChargeForPayment` after the mint, and the `pos_charge_invoice` read in the insert transaction; logs `pay.invoice_failed`, and a held hash also `shop.alert.duplicate_invoice`); 503 `stablecoin_unavailable` when `findChargeForPayment` fails with an error (nothing written), when no stored spot has a USD rate younger than 10 minutes (no quote is requested), when no quote passes the offer checks (no pair commits), or when the insert transaction finds the request 16 s old or older or its client disconnected (order 9, nothing written).
- **Used by:** The app's payment page for an entered amount; it falls back to `POST /pay/:username/invoice` on 503 and on other 4xx, and reloads the till charge on 409 `payment_open`.
- **Auth:** none.

## Endpoint: POST /lnurl/pay-request

- **Purpose:** Fetches and checks the LNURL pay request of a Lightning Address or bech32 LNURL on another host, for addresses the app cannot read from the browser (no CORS). Body `{ target }`; an optional `lightning:` prefix (any case) is dropped and the target is lowercased; a bech32 LNURL in mixed case is refused. The URL must be `https`, on a DNS name of at most 253 characters with at least two labels (no address literal, no `localhost`, no `.local` / `.internal` / `.localhost`, no trailing dot), with no port other than the default 443 and no user name or password, and every address it resolves to must pass `isPublicIp` (public unicast only, IPv4 carried in IPv6 checked as IPv4), and an IPv6 answer must also lie in `2000::/3` or the well-known NAT64 prefix `64:ff9b::/96` and not in `2001::/23`. A Lightning Address whose name is only dots is refused. The fetch does not follow redirects and reads at most 64 KB; each lookup and each fetch waits at most 5 s and the whole call at most 8 s (below the server's 10 s idle timeout). The pay request needs `tag: 'payRequest'`, an `https` callback that passes the same host checks, a string `metadata`, safe-integer `minSendable` ≥ 1 and `maxSendable` ≥ `minSendable`, and an optional non-negative integer `commentAllowed` (missing or null is 0). Response `{ target, minSendableMsat, maxSendableMsat, commentAllowed, description, domain }`; `description` is the metadata's `text/plain` entry or an empty string.
- **Errors:** 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` without a session; 429 `{ error: 'Too many requests' }` with `Retry-After: 60` over 30 relay requests per member per minute (shared with `POST /lnurl/invoice`); 400 `{ error: 'Not a payable address' }` for a missing or malformed target (including one over 2048 characters), a target on the host of `PUBLIC_BASE_URL`, a target or callback host that resolves to a non-public address, or a pay request that fails the checks; 404 `{ error: 'Address not found' }` for HTTP 404/410 or an LNURL `ERROR` body; 502 `{ error: 'Address could not be reached' }` for a host that does not resolve in time, a step or the whole call running out of time, a network failure, timeout, redirect, other non-2xx status, an oversize body, or a body that is not a JSON object.
- **Used by:** The app when a member pays a Lightning Address or LNURL on another host with the in-app wallet.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session.

## Endpoint: POST /lnurl/invoice

- **Purpose:** Resolves `target` again (same checks as `POST /lnurl/pay-request`; the client never sends a callback URL), checks `amountMsat` and `comment`, and requests a BOLT11 from the callback with `amount` and, when non-empty, `comment`. Body `{ target, amountMsat, comment? }`. The invoice must decode to exactly `amountMsat` and carry a description hash equal to SHA-256 of the pay request's `metadata`. Response `{ pr }`. Success logs `lnurl.invoice.ok` with `amountMsat`; failure logs `lnurl.invoice.failed` with `reason` and `status`; the target, comment, and invoice are never logged.
- **Errors:** As `POST /lnurl/pay-request`, plus 400 `{ error: 'Amount out of range' }` when `amountMsat` is not a number, not a whole number of millisatoshis, or outside `[minSendable, maxSendable]`; 400 `{ error: 'Comment too long' }` when `comment` is not a string (null is no comment), is not well-formed Unicode (a lone surrogate) or longer than 2000 UTF-16 code units (both refused before any outbound request), or has more characters than `commentAllowed`; 502 `{ error: 'Address could not be reached' }` when the invoice fetch fails or the invoice is missing, undecodable, for another amount, or for another description hash.
- **Used by:** The app before it pays the returned `pr` with the in-app wallet.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session.

## Endpoint: GET /apple-touch-icon.png

- **Purpose:** PNG brand mark (apple-touch). `Cache-Control: public, max-age=86400`.
- **Errors:** 404 empty body when `public/apple-touch-icon.png` is missing.
- **Used by:** iOS home-screen icon crawlers.
- **Auth:** See Purpose — Bearer where stated, else public.

## Endpoint: GET /debug/accounts

- **Purpose:** Operator listing of registered accounts. Same shape as `serializeDebugAccount`: public fields including `username`, plus `isPlatform`, `sessionRefused`, `viewKey`, skip stamps, `profileMessageId`, `notificationLevel`, `notifyHearts` (boolean, default true), `amountUnit`, `locale` (nullable, null when unset, owner/debug only, omitted from public member cards and view profiles), `fiat` (nullable, null when unset, owner/debug only, omitted from public member cards and view profiles), `walletRequired`, `walletBackupSeenAt`, `walletIntroSeen`, `sparkPubkey`, `sparkPubkeyVerifiedAt` (null until claimed / verified), and Nostr debug fields (`nostrNsecCiphertext` is hex of the stored envelope, never decrypted).
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match.
- **Used by:** Operator `gifts-debug` CLI.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: POST /debug/accounts

- **Purpose:** Operator provision of accounts by display name (no passkey, no wallet, `rulesAgreedAt` null). Body `{ "accounts": [ { "name", "username"? } ] }` (1–100 rows; unknown keys such as `lightningAddress` are refused). With `username`, an existing account with that username (`lower(trim)`) gets only its `name` updated (`created: false`; other columns stay) and otherwise a new `basis` row with that username and a fresh `viewKey` is created. Without `username`, every row creates a new `basis` row with a username derived from the name (`provisionUsername`). A provisioned member has no verified wallet, so it can read but cannot receive or post until it sets one up. Response `{ accounts: [ { name, username, viewKey, created } ] }` includes `viewKey` for the invite link. `GET /debug/accounts` and `GET /debug/accounts/:id` also include `viewKey` and `username`.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 400 `{ error: 'Expected a JSON body with an "accounts" array' }` for an invalid/missing/non-JSON body, an unknown row key, C0/DEL names, or an invalid username (no row is written); 500 `{ error: 'Could not save the account' }` when a created row cannot be read back.
- **Used by:** Operator provisioning before passkey claim.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: GET /debug/accounts/:id

- **Purpose:** Operator read of one account (`serializeDebugAccountDetail`): the account columns except the legacy `lightning_address` and `lightning_address_verified` and the derived `spark_address` (it follows from `sparkPubkey`), plus nested `passkeys`, `sessions`, and matching `passkeyChallenges`. `lightningAddress` is the receiving address (verified wallet) when the LNURL server is configured. `nostrNsecCiphertext` is hex of the stored AES-GCM envelope, never decrypted. Nested `sessions[].token` is the stored plaintext token.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` when the id is not a UUID or the account is missing.
- **Used by:** Operator `gifts-debug account`.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: POST /debug/accounts/:id/session

- **Purpose:** Operator mint of a member bearer session for the given account id. Response `{ token }`. For e2e and operator debugging only — not a member login path. An account with `sessionRefused` is refused with no mint and no `debug.accounts.session_minted` log. Not checked against the login switch or `APP_BUILD_REQUIRED`: it is an operator tool, not a member login.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` when the account id is unknown; 403 `{ error: 'You signed in with the wrong account. Please try again with the correct account.' }` when `sessionRefused` is true.
- **Used by:** Playwright e2e against the booted process; operators reproducing member HTTP.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: POST /debug/accounts/merge

- **Purpose:** Operator merge of account `from` into account `into`, in one database transaction. Body `{ "from", "into", "verify"? }`. Both ids are UUIDs. `verify` is `from` or `into` and defaults to `into`. Success is 200 `{ into, deleted, messages }`: `deleted` is the source id, `messages` is how many message rows belonged to `from` before the move. The survivor is the account named by `into`. Its name, username, location, and in-app wallet key stay, including the wallet verification. Gifts are stored against the Wallet of Satoshi username and are not moved. Nostr keys, the view key, and the linking key stay. `sessionRefused` stays the survivor's: a refusal on the closed account does not lock the login that remains. Notification level and amount unit stay the survivor's, because those columns have no empty value. Locale and fiat stay when the survivor already has one, and are copied from the source only when the survivor's value is empty. `created_at` becomes the earlier of the two times, so a replacement login does not look like a new person. `rules_agreed_at` becomes the earlier agreement; if only one account agreed, that time is kept; if neither agreed, it stays empty. `wallet_required` becomes true when either account already has it, so a closed recovery-phrase prompt is not opened again. `wallet_backup_seen_at` becomes the earlier time, or the only time when just one account has seen it. `forum_laws_dismissed` becomes true when either account dismissed the laws. `wallet_intro_seen` becomes true when either account saw the wallet intro. Role stays the survivor's and is never downgraded; a basis survivor becomes verified when a verify edge remains. The profile note stays the survivor's when it has one, and is the source profile note only when the survivor has none. `verify: "into"` keeps the survivor's verify edge and deletes the source's. `verify: "from"` deletes the survivor's verify edge and moves the source's onto the survivor. Passkeys of both accounts stay, so both phones can sign in. Sessions of the source are deleted, not moved. The source's social recovery rows are deleted, not moved: its `recovery_share` rows, its `pending_recovery` row, its `previous_wallet` rows, and its `recovery` passkey challenges. They belong to the source's wallets, and a share's additional data is the source id. Messages, contacts, and push rows are moved. A direct conversation that exists only between the two accounts is deleted. Two conversations with the same other party are collapsed onto the survivor's conversation, and the messages are kept. The same slot of a profile image, the same notification, the same repayment day, and a second open till charge keep the survivor's row and drop the source's. When both accounts have a free first post, the source note moves without its free mark. A second funding grant refuses the merge. A source account with a shop payment that is neither `paid` nor closed refuses the merge, because its BOLT11 still pays the source wallet; a source with an unexpired pending charge while the survivor has an `open` shop payment and no unexpired pending charge of its own refuses it the same way, before any write, so a till charge never sits next to another payment's open amount; paid and closed shop payments move like every other row. When the survivor has a pending charge (expired or not), the source's pending charges are deleted: all of the source's charge rows are locked first in the order of their ids (`SELECT id … ORDER BY id FOR UPDATE`), the pending charges among them are read, the source's shop payment rows are locked the same way, and then their `pos_charge_invoice` rows and the charges are deleted by those ids; a concurrent `recordInvoice` for such a charge waits on the lock and then fails (`pos.invoice.record_failed` in the invoice routes), and a later one records nothing (`not_open`). A throw rolls every write back.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 503 `{ error: 'Merge is unavailable' }` when `mergeDb` is omitted or the merge throws (including SQLSTATE 23505); 400 `{ error: 'Expected a JSON body with "from" and "into"' }` for a missing, non-JSON, or invalid body; 409 `{ error: 'Cannot merge an account into itself' }` when `from` and `into` are the same id; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` when either account is missing; 409 `{ error: 'Cannot merge the platform account' }` when either account is the platform account; 409 `{ error: 'Both accounts have a funding grant' }` when both accounts have a funding grant; 409 `{ error: 'A payment is already open' }` when the source account has a shop payment that is neither paid nor closed, or an unexpired pending charge while the survivor has an `open` shop payment and no unexpired pending charge (`shop_payment_open`); when the survivor has a pending charge (expired or not), the source's pending charges are deleted and the merge goes on.
- **Used by:** Operator debug token. Not a member session.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: POST /debug/shop-proofs/:id/retry

- **Purpose:** Operator retry of a shop payment proof that is `stuck` (`debugShopProofsRoutes`, `retryStuckProof`). Mounted at `/debug/shop-proofs` only when `LNURL_SERVER_URL` and `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` resolve. Moves the proof back to unresolved, sets `give_up_at` 24 h ahead, and logs `shop.proof.retried`. A `hex` proof without a log index gets `next_receipt_at` now, so the receipts duty reads and sends its transaction again and the submits duty submits it once the receipt filled the log index; any other proof gets `next_submit_at` now and the shop payment watcher submits it again. 200 `{ id, result: null }`. No body.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for an id that is not a UUID, an unknown proof, or when the route is not mounted; 409 `{ error: 'The proof is not stuck' }` for a proof in any other state.
- **Used by:** Operator after `shop.alert.proof_stuck`.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: PATCH /debug/accounts/:id

- **Purpose:** Operator assignment of `account.role` (`basis` \| `verified` \| `moderator` \| `initiator` \| `founder`), the official platform flag, and/or `sessionRefused`. Body may include any of `{ "role": "<AccountRole>" }`, `{ "platform": true|false }`, `{ "sessionRefused": true|false }`; unknown keys (including `lightningAddress`) are refused. Setting `platform: true` clears any other platform flag (at most one true) and, when a conversation store is wired, points every `member_platform` thread at this account (`retargetMemberPlatform`), except a thread whose member is already this account. `sessionRefused: true` makes passkey finish and debug session mint return 403 with the wrong-account copy. Returns the updated account JSON (same shape as `GET /debug/accounts` via `serializeDebugAccount`, including `isPlatform`, `sessionRefused`, `viewKey`, `walletRequired`, `walletBackupSeenAt`, `walletIntroSeen`, `sparkPubkey`, `sparkPubkeyVerifiedAt` (null until claimed / verified), and Nostr debug fields).
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 400 `{ error: 'Expected a JSON body with a "role" string, platform boolean, and/or sessionRefused boolean' }` for an unknown key or a missing/non-JSON body; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` when the account id is unknown.
- **Used by:** Operator `gifts-debug role` / `gifts-debug refuse-session` CLI and platform-account setup. Does not write trust edges (`POST /debug/trust-edges` is the backfill path).
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: GET /debug/db

- **Purpose:** Operator read of every ordinary table in schema `public`. With no `table`, returns `{ tables: [{ name, rowCount }] }` sorted by name. With `table`, returns one page of 200 rows (`columns`, `rows`) and `nextCursor` when another page exists. Follow `nextCursor` until it is absent to read the whole table. `bytea` values, including `nostr_nsec_ciphertext` and `recovery_share_ciphertext`, are octet lengths (or null), never the bytes. Text columns named `token`, `challenge`, `nonce`, `view_key`, `endpoint`, `p256dh`, `auth`, `delivered_endpoints`, `skip_endpoints`, or `session_token` are the string `"redacted"` when not null. A primary key that is one of those secret columns is paged by `ctid`, so `nextCursor` is not the secret. There is no `limit` that stops early.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` only when `DEBUG_TOKEN` and `DEBUG_READ_TOKEN` are both empty after trim; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the bearer matches no configured token; 503 `{ error: 'Database is not configured' }` when this process has no SQL client; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` when `table` is not an ordinary public table; 400 `{ error: 'Invalid cursor' }` when `cursor` is present without `table` or does not match the table key; 503 `{ error: 'Database is unavailable' }` if the store throws (`debug.db.failed`).
- **Wallet and interaction tables:** Lists `wallet_balance_snapshot`, `wallet_payment`, and `member_event` like any other table, unredacted (columns and `category` values in SPEC `GET /debug/db`). A payment between two members has two `wallet_payment` rows, the payer's `out` row and the payee's `in` row, with possibly different `payment_id` values but, when the apps reported it, the same `payment_hash` or Spark `invoice`; a total over all members must keep one row per payment (for example by deduplicating on `payment_hash` or `invoice`), since summing every row counts it twice.
- **Used by:** Operators reading the whole database (`gifts-debug db`).
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN` or `DEBUG_READ_TOKEN`. `DEBUG_READ_TOKEN` applies to GET and HEAD `/debug/db` and otherwise only to the operator reads of `GET /funding/goal`, `GET /funding/payout-days`, `GET /funding/applications`, `GET /funding/applications/:accountId`, `GET /funding/daily-roster`, `GET /funding/daily-roster/links`, `GET /trust/proposals`, `GET /members/:accountId/activity`, `GET /messages/hidden`, and `GET /messages/:id/edits`. HEAD is the same GET handler. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: GET /debug/api-log

- **Purpose:** Operator listing of HTTP audit rows newest-first, one keyset page of 200. Follow `before` (last log `createdAt`) and `beforeId` (its `id`) while `hasMore` is true to read every matching row, including every row for one `accountId`. Omit `accountId` for every account. Body `{ logs, hasMore }`: method, redacted path, status, ms, nullable `accountId` (always present; JSON `null` unless `authKind` is `session`), `authKind` (`session` | `debug` | `spend` | `none`), and nullable `clientIp`, `clientCountry`, `cfRay`, `userAgent`, `acceptLanguage`, and `origin` (JSON `null` when the header is missing or invalid). `createdAt` is ISO-8601. The `api_log` dump is the newest 200; the full history is this endpoint. No query string, Authorization, bodies, or tokens. OPTIONS and `/healthz` are not stored.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 400 `{ error: 'Invalid account' }` when `accountId` is present and not a UUID; 400 `{ error: 'Invalid cursor' }` when only one of `before` / `beforeId` is present, `before` is not a finite `Date.parse` time, or `beforeId` is not a UUID; 503 `{ error: 'Log is unavailable' }` if the store throws (`api_log.list.failed`).
- **Used by:** Operators attributing who called the API (`gifts-debug api-log`).
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: POST /diagnostics

- **Purpose:** Public client diagnostic ingest with no auth. The JSON object requires `event` (`client.` plus 1–60 of `a-z`, digits, and `.`). Optional `name` (1–40 letters), `message` (1–120 of letters, digits, `.`, `_`, `:`, space, `-`; no slash), `prfPresent` (boolean, never the bytes), `challengeId` (64 lowercase hex), `accountId` (UUID), `stage` (`register` / `authenticate` / `seed` / `login` / `unhandled`), `status` (integer 100–599), and `path` (string; `/view/<segment>` becomes `/view/:viewKey`; rejected if it contains `?` or 32 lowercase hex digits (`0-9`, `a-f`) in a row). Any other key is rejected. A valid body is stored as a `client` row and answered with 204 and an empty body. `User-Agent` is not a body field; the server may store it as `userAgent` after stripping controls and truncating to 200. The server also stores `clientIp`, `clientCountry`, `cfRay`, `acceptLanguage`, and `origin` when those headers validate, using the same rules as the audit log; those names are not body keys, and absent values are omitted. Rows are kept forever (no TTL, no DELETE). Secrets are not stored: no PRF bytes, mnemonic, session token, view key, nsec, Authorization, Cookie, WebAuthn challenge, attestation, signatures, or raw request bodies.
- **Errors:** 400 `{ error: 'Invalid diagnostics' }` when JSON or any field fails the allowlist; 429 `{ error: 'Too many diagnostics' }` when the IP window (60) or the global window (600) in 60 seconds is full (the accept timestamp is not recorded on 429); 500 `{ error: 'Log is unavailable' }` when the insert throws (that failure does not consume a rate-limit slot).
- **Auth:** None. No session and no debug bearer.

## Endpoint: GET /debug/diagnostics

- **Purpose:** Operator read of diagnostic rows, newest-first, cap 200, with `createdAt` as ISO-8601 and allowlisted scalar fields. Rows are kept forever (no TTL, no DELETE). No PRF bytes, mnemonic, session token, view key, nsec, Authorization, Cookie, WebAuthn challenge, attestation, signatures, or request bodies.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 503 `{ error: 'Log is unavailable' }` if the store throws.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: GET /debug/contacts

- **Purpose:** Operator listing of private in-app contacts newest-first (cap 200), including `accountId`, name snapshot, text, and ISO `createdAt`.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 503 `{ error: 'Contact is unavailable' }` if the store throws (`contact.list.failed`).
- **Used by:** Operators reading the private mailbox.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: GET /debug/invoices

- **Purpose:** Operator listing of all `message_invoice` attempts (forum `POST /messages/:id/invoice` and conversation `POST /conversations/:id/invoice`) newest-first (cap 200): result, HTTP status, BOLT11 `pr`, payment hash, description / description_hash, `isNip57Invoice`, and `lnurlResponse` (raw LNURL callback JSON object or null). ISO `createdAt`. Never includes nsec. Includes `conversationId` and `conversationMessageId` (`null` on forum invoices), plus `fiatPinned` and `amountUsd`, `amountChf`, `amountEur`, and `amountPhp` (`null` when unset). Rejected non-NIP-57 attempts (`not_zap`) still list the rejected `pr` for debug.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` when listing throws (`debug.invoices.list_failed`).
- **Used by:** Operators debugging zap invoice issuance (including rejected non-NIP-57 `not_zap` rows with `pr` and raw `lnurlResponse`).
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: POST /debug/invoices/settle

- **Purpose:** Manually settle one successful forum `message_invoice` by its 32-byte payment hash. Body `{ paymentHash, note, preimage? }`; `note` is trimmed operator evidence (1–8000 characters, no C0/DEL controls). A verified optional preimage is stored in the synthetic kind:9735 tags, but is not required because wallet-internal payments can expose a value that does not hash to the invoice payment hash.
- **Trust / effects:** `DEBUG_TOKEN` plus the required durable note is the settlement authority; when supplied, `preimage` must be 32-byte hex and hash to `paymentHash`. Success claims the payment hash, credits `message.sats` once, writes an `indexed` `nostr_zap_ingest` with `manual=debug-settle` and `note`. On a member note, fans out `notifyZap` and inserts the payer gift-reply, except a heart invoice (`heart === true`): sats are still credited, `notifyZap` and the gift-reply are skipped, and `notifyHeart` notifies only the note author. On the platform profile note, skips `notifyZap` and treats the zap comment as a compose post/reply (`sats` 0) gated by `forum.post`; a created top-level post fans out `notifyForumPost` and `spendPing` only when `eligibleToday` (same gate as `POST /messages`), a reply fans out `notifyForumReply`. The response contains only `{ receiptId, messageId, amountSats, resumed }`, never the note or preimage. If credit succeeded but that direct ingest write failed, a retry writes the missing ingest from the current request, runs the post-credit effects, returns `resumed: true`, and does not credit again.
- **Errors:** 400 `Invalid body` for malformed JSON/types, 400 `Invalid note`, 400 for malformed/mismatched hash or preimage, 404 for a missing invoice/message, 409 for conversation invoices or an already indexed/settled payment, and 503 `Messages are unavailable` when a store operation throws, including the ingest write, or a thrown note-author lookup before claim. A later relay receipt for the same hash is recorded as `rejected`/`settled` and cannot add sats again because the payment-hash claim survives ingest failures and message deletion.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`; unset/blank returns 503 and a bad bearer returns 401. This operator-only authority is the same debug token used for role assignment and session minting.
- **Used by:** `gifts-debug settle <payment-hash> <note> [preimage]` after the normal zap-receipt window has elapsed.

## Endpoint: POST /debug/spend-ping

- **Purpose:** Replay one qualifying top-level forum post onto that post's UTC day (`utcDayKey` of `createdAt`), not the clock's UTC day. Body `{ messageId }` (UUID). An earlier UTC day does not consume the clock day's payout slot. A post from the clock's UTC day still books that day. An already-paid post day comes back skipped. The future-day check runs only after the hidden, reply, profile, account, receiving address, eligibility, and media gates. The process calls `spendPing.pingOnDay(address, message.id, day, effectiveStatus(grant, now))` only when the note is live, not a reply, not the account profile note, the account has a receiving address (verified wallet, sent as `address`), the payout gate (`loadPayoutEligibility`: `eligibleToday` with the grant and, from the grant gate day, the 21.gifts recovery share of the current verified wallet) passes on the clock (not the post's day), and the note has media (`hasPhoto`, `hasVideo`, or `photoCount` > 0). `day` is that post's UTC day. Accepted is 202 `{ messageId, status: 'accepted' }`. Skipped is 200 `{ messageId, status: 'skipped', reason }`. The address is never returned.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 503 `{ error: 'Spend ping is not configured' }` when `spendPing` is absent or `pingOnDay` is missing; 400 `{ error: 'Invalid body' }` for malformed JSON, a non-object, or a non-UUID `messageId`; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` when the message is missing; 404 `{ error: 'Account not found' }` when `accountId` is null or the account is missing; 409 `{ error: 'Message is hidden' }`, `{ error: 'Replies are not paid' }`, `{ error: 'Profile notes are not paid' }`, `{ error: 'Message is not from today' }`, `{ error: 'No Lightning address' }`, or `{ error: 'Message has no media' }`; 403 `{ error: 'Not eligible' }` when the payout gate fails on the clock, including a member in the grant program without a recovery share (no ping); 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` when `getById`, `getAccount`, `getByAccountId`, or the recovery share read throws, when `pingOnDay` throws, or when `pingOnDay` returns `{ status: 'failed' }` (`debug.spend_ping.failed`). Skips log `debug.spend_ping.skipped` with `reason` `not_eligible` or `no_media`. An accepted ping logs `debug.spend_ping.sent`. Logs carry `messageId` only, never the address.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.
- **Window body:** `{ from, to, dryRun? }` (instants read by `parseClientInstant`: ISO-8601 or epoch, not before 2009, at most 5 minutes after the clock; `to` exclusive; at most 48 hours; `dryRun` boolean, default false) replays every payout trigger in that window through `replayPayoutWindow`: new top-level media posts, moderator-group messages, `/trial` and `/admit` decisions, and welcome triggers. Each daily payout or moderator stipend is booked on the event's own UTC day, once per member, kind, and day, and only for an account that has a verified wallet and is eligible now; an event of a member whose `trial` grant is of an earlier UTC day, also once it was stored as `pending` (a `/trial` decision, or a post or moderator message on that day) comes back `not_eligible` / `trial_day_passed` with the username and event, for a payout by hand; an account that is eligible except for the 21.gifts recovery share of its current verified wallet comes back `not_eligible` / `no_recovery_share`; any other refusal, including a `basis` account with such a trial, which is never eligible, comes back `not_eligible` / `grant`. 200 `{ from, to, dryRun, events, totals }` (`totals` is `{ events, paid, wouldPay, sent, wouldSend, skipped, failed }`, the number of events in all and per status); each event is `paid`, `would_pay`, `sent` (welcome handed to the welcome path), `would_send`, `skipped` (`reason` `no_wallet`, `already_paid`, `not_eligible`, or `spend`, with `detail`), or `failed`. 400 `Invalid body` for an instant `parseClientInstant` refuses, an empty window (`from` not before `to`), a window longer than 48 hours, a `dryRun` that is not a boolean, or a window that also has `messageId`; 503 `Spend ping is not configured` without `pingOnDay`; 503 `Messages are unavailable` when a store read throws (`debug.spend_ping.replay_failed`). A replay logs `debug.spend_ping.replayed` with `dryRun` and the totals, never an address. Running it again over the same window is safe: spend's record per day and address returns what it already booked as `already_paid` / `spend`. Dry run pings nothing. `would_send` and `sent` mean handed to the welcome path, which itself skips an account that was already welcomed.
- **Used by:** Operators replaying a qualifying living-room post that never reached spend, or every payout trigger of a window in which spend was not paying.

## Endpoint: GET /debug/welcome-sweep

- **Purpose:** Operator dry-run of the 15-minute welcome sweep. Returns `{ enabled, spendPing, wouldPing }`: `enabled` is the sweep switch, `spendPing` is whether a spend ping is configured (without one the sweep pings nobody), and `wouldPing` lists `{ accountId, username, messageId }` for every account the sweep would welcome-ping now (`welcomeSweepCandidates`: `verified`, verified wallet, live top-level photo or video, and not `accountWelcomed`). Pings nobody and writes nothing. The wallet address is not returned. Logs `debug.welcome_sweep.listed` with the count.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` when a store read throws (`debug.welcome_sweep.list_failed`).
- **Used by:** Operators before turning the sweep on: `wouldPing` must be empty or hold only accounts that are owed the welcome gift.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: PUT /debug/welcome-sweep

- **Purpose:** Operator set of the welcome sweep switch. Body `{ enabled: boolean }` (no other keys). The switch is process memory shared with the boot timer; every boot starts with it off, so the sweep (`syncWelcomePing` without an account, on boot and every 15 minutes) pings nobody until an operator turns it on; turning it off stops a running sweep before its next ping. Single-account welcome pings after a post, an About-me save, or a verification are not affected. Returns `{ enabled }`. Logs `debug.welcome_sweep.set`.
- **Errors:** 503 Debug is not configured; 401 Unauthorized; 400 `{ error: 'Expected a JSON body with an "enabled" boolean' }`.
- **Used by:** Operators turning the welcome sweep on after `GET /debug/welcome-sweep` showed the expected list, and off again.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: GET /debug/zap-ingests

- **Purpose:** Operator listing of kind:9735 ingest decisions newest-first (cap 200): `outcome` (`indexed` \| `rejected`), `reason`, receipt id, note/message ids, amount, and the receipt event frame. ISO `createdAt`. Never includes nsec. One `nostr_zap_ingest` row is written per receipt per decision change per process (the memory is per store instance and empty after a restart, so the first ingest pass after boot may write one `rejected`/`duplicate` row per receipt that pass still queries (`listLatest` plus non-null `listReplies` children of those rows, plus the newest MESSAGE_LIST_LIMIT live replies that have an event id (`listRecentReplies`) even when their parent is not in `listLatest`, unioned with the official platform profile note's `eventId` even after it ages out of `listLatest`)). A repeated identical `outcome:reason` is normally not written again, because the memory is consulted before the write; that is not a guarantee, since the memory is set only after the write resolves, fast-lane ticks are not serialised, and a failed write leaves it untouched. Receipts whose remembered decision is terminal (`indexed` or `rejected`/`duplicate`) skip note lookup, account/LNURL validation, and ingest persist, but still run `verifyReceipt` then `tryEnsureGiftReply`.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` when listing throws (`debug.zap_ingests.list_failed`).
- **Used by:** Operators debugging zap receipt indexing.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: GET /debug/messages

- **Purpose:** Operator listing of every persisted forum row newest-first (cap 200): top-level notes **and** replies, live **and** soft-hidden (`deletedAt` set). Public hide does **not** apply. JSON via `serializeDebugMessage` (`id`, `name`, `text`, ISO `createdAt`, `sats`, `goalSats` (stored column; JSON `null` when unset), `goalRepayable` only when stored true (omitted when null, never false), `goalTermDays` only when stored (omitted when null), `goalCurrency`, `goalAmount`, and `goalAmountUsd` / `goalAmountChf` / `goalAmountEur` / `goalAmountPhp` when `goalCurrency` is stored (omitted on a legacy row; a snapshot may be null), `placeLat`, `placeLng`, `placeLabel`, `placeStreet`, `placeHouseNumber`, `placePostalCode`, `placeCity`, `placeCountry` (JSON `null` when unset), `hasPhoto`, `photoCount` (0–10), `photoTakenAts` (always; length equals `photoCount`; null when unknown; `[]` when there are no stills), `photoTakenAt` only when `photoCount === 1`, `hasVideo`, `videoContentType`, `parentId`, `eventId`, `nostrPublishState`, `nostrEvent`, `claimedUntil`, `nostrFirstAttemptAt`, `nostrPublishEpoch`, `contentFp`, ISO-or-null `deletedAt`, `deletedBy`, `authorPubkey`, `nostrAttempts`, `accountId` as a string or JSON `null` for Damus-only, plus photo MIME/byte lengths). Never includes nsec or photo/video payloads.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` when listing throws (`debug.messages.list_failed`).
- **Used by:** Operators inspecting hidden forum notes (`gifts-debug messages`).
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: GET /debug/messages/:id

- **Purpose:** Operator single-note fetch (Bearer `DEBUG_TOKEN`). Returns the debug JSON object (not wrapped) via `serializeDebugMessage` (same columns as `GET /debug/messages`, including `nostrEvent`, `claimedUntil`, `contentFp`, photo MIME/byte lengths, stored `goalSats` as JSON `null` when unset, `goalRepayable` only when stored true (omitted when null, never false), `goalTermDays` only when stored (omitted when null), the currency-ask keys when `goalCurrency` is stored, and `placeLat` / `placeLng` / `placeLabel` / `placeStreet` / `placeHouseNumber` / `placePostalCode` / `placeCity` / `placeCountry` as JSON `null` when unset).
