Soft-hidden rows (`deletedAt` set) are **200** with `deletedAt` / `deletedBy` / `text`. Public `GET /messages/:id` hide does **not** apply. Unknown or non-UUID `:id` is 404. Never includes nsec or photo/video payloads.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` when `:id` is not a UUID or the row is missing; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` when `getById` or serialize throws (`debug.messages.get_failed`).
- **Used by:** Operators fetching one forum note including hidden rows (`gifts-debug message <id>`).
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: GET /debug/messages/:id/photo

- **Purpose:** Operator JPEG/PNG/WebP bytes for a forum note, **including** soft-hidden rows. Same `Content-Type` / `Content-Disposition` / CORS as public `GET /messages/:id/photo`. Public hide does **not** apply: a hidden note with a photo is 200. Missing row, no photo, or non-UUID `:id` is 404. Never returns photo bytes inside JSON.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 404 `{ error: 'Photo not found' }` when `:id` is not a UUID, the row is missing, or `getPhoto` returns null; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` when the store throws (`debug.messages.photo.get_failed`).
- **Used by:** Operators viewing a hidden forum photo without SSH.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: GET /debug/messages/:id/photo/:file

- **Purpose:** Operator extra still (indices 1–9) **including** soft-hidden rows. Hidden with that extra is **200**. Same headers as public extra / photo 0. `:file` same regex as public.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 404 `{ error: 'Photo not found' }` when `:id` is not a UUID, the row is missing, `:file` is invalid, or `getExtraPhoto` returns null; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` (`debug.messages.photo.get_failed`).
- **Used by:** Operators viewing a hidden extra still without SSH.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: PUT /debug/messages/:id/video

- **Purpose:** Operator restore of missing forum-video bytes for an existing message with `hasVideo`. Raw body is validated (`decodeForumVideo`), must match the stored MIME extension, and is written under `MEDIA_DIR` so public `GET /messages/:id/video.*` can serve it. Does not create a new message id or change the DB row.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match (checked before the body is read); 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for a non-UUID or unknown id; 409 `{ error: 'Message has no video' }` when `hasVideo` is not true or `videoContentType` is missing; 409 `{ error: 'Video type does not match' }` when the decoded type's extension differs from the stored MIME; 400 `{ error: 'Expected a video body' }` for empty, oversize, or unrecognized bytes; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` when the store or disk write throws (`debug.messages.video.put_failed`).
- **Used by:** Operators restoring a missing on-disk forum video without SSH (`gifts-debug video-put`).
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: POST /debug/messages/:id/restore

- **Purpose:** Operator unhide of a soft-hidden forum note (Bearer `DEBUG_TOKEN`). Calls `MessageStore.markUndeleted`: inverse of `markDeleted`'s cascade (clears `deletedAt` / `deletedBy` on the hidden target and stamp-matched **direct** children; already-live target is a no-op for children). After that succeeds, calls `unblockPubkeyByMessage`, which removes a block only when this id is the source row whose hide created it. Restoring that source row unblocks the pubkey but leaves unrelated cascade-hidden rows hidden; restoring any other row hidden by the external-pubkey cascade makes that row visible but leaves the pubkey blocked, so each such row must be restored separately and new content from the still-blocked pubkey continues to be rejected. Does not recreate the row via `POST /messages`, does not hard-delete, and does not unlink media, invoices, zap receipts, Nostr, text, or photo. Existing live id still 204. Success is 204 empty body. After a successful unhide, a note that is now a public shop gets its identity when needed and its durable place outbox is queued; delivery failures never change the 204. When the note is a public shop after the unhide, the photo outbox is queued as well. Logs `debug.messages.restored` with `{ messageId }` only (never text, never `deletedBy`).
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for a non-UUID or unknown id; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` when `markUndeleted` or `unblockPubkeyByMessage` throws (`debug.messages.restore_failed`).
- **Used by:** Operators unhiding a soft-hidden forum note (`gifts-debug restore`).
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: GET /push/vapid-public

- **Purpose:** Bearer session. Returns `{ publicKey }` (URL-safe base64 VAPID public) so the app can subscribe.
- **Errors:** 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` without a session; 503 `{ error: 'Push is not configured' }` when VAPID keys are missing.
- **Used by:** App `fetchVapidPublicKey` / enable-notifications.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` member session.

## Endpoint: POST /me/push-subscriptions

- **Purpose:** Bearer session. Upserts `{ endpoint, keys: { p256dh, auth } }` for the account and records the bearer session that posted it. Rebinds the endpoint (account and session) if another account or session held it. The push worker sends to the endpoint only while that session is still valid and deletes it once the session expires, is removed, or its account is refused sessions; the app's start-up re-POST binds it to the current session.
- **Errors:** 401 Unauthorized; 503 Push is not configured; 400 `{ error: 'Invalid subscription' }`.
- **Used by:** App `postPushSubscription`.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` member session.

## Endpoint: DELETE /me/push-subscriptions

- **Purpose:** Bearer session. Body `{ endpoint }` removes that device for the account.
- **Errors:** 401 Unauthorized; 503 Push is not configured; 400 Invalid subscription; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }`.
- **Used by:** App `deletePushSubscription`.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` member session.

## Endpoint: POST /debug/push-ping

- **Purpose:** Operator enqueue of a test notification for `{ accountId }`. Returns `{ enqueued }` (`0` or `1`).
- **Errors:** 503 Debug is not configured; 401 Unauthorized; 503 Push is not configured; 400 expected accountId; 404 Not found.
- **Used by:** Operators verifying Web Push delivery.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: POST /webhooks/lnurl

- **Purpose:** The self-hosted LNURL server calls it once per settled lightning address invoice (`spark_payment_received`). The receiver (the account whose verified wallet is `data.user_pubkey`) gets one Web Push `You received <amount_sat> sats.` that opens `/wallet`, titled with the sender's name when a signed zap request names the receiver and this amount and its signer is a 21.gifts account with a name, otherwise `21.gifts`. A zap on a forum note gets this push as well; the zap notification about the note is separate. Returns 200 `{ outcome }`: `queued`, `duplicate` (this payment hash was already pushed), `unknown` (no verified wallet), `self` (the receiver signed the zap request), `off` (notification level `none`), `no_device` (no push subscription whose login session is still live), or `ignored` (a body above 64 KiB, or a signed body that is not a usable `spark_payment_received`; nothing is written).
- **Mounted:** Only when `LNURL_WEBHOOK_SECRET` is set; otherwise 404.
- **Auth:** `X-Breez-Signature` must be the hex HMAC-SHA256 of the raw body with `LNURL_WEBHOOK_SECRET` (the `domain_webhooks.webhook_secret` of the LNURL server). No session.
- **Errors:** 401 `Unauthorized`; 503 `Notifications are unavailable` when a store call fails, so the LNURL server retries. The claim of the payment hash and the outbox row are written together, so a retry never pushes twice.
- **Used by:** The LNURL server's webhook delivery (retried with backoff on any non-2xx answer).

## Endpoint: POST /debug/push-broadcast

- **Purpose:** Operator Web Push broadcast. Body `{ title, body, link?, usernames?, dryRun? }`. Without `usernames` it targets every account with a live push subscription (its session still authenticates the account, the rule the push worker sends by); with `usernames` (1-200) it targets those accounts. Accounts whose notification level is `none` are counted as `optedOut` and listed accounts without a live subscription as `noSubscription`; neither is queued. Each recipient gets one push outbox row, delivered by the push worker with the usual batch, lease, and retry rules. Returns 202 `{ id, targeted, queued, optedOut, noSubscription }`; `link` defaults to `/welcome`.
- **Dry run:** `dryRun: true` returns 200 `{ dryRun: true, recipients }` and queues nothing; it works without VAPID.
- **Validation:** strict keys; lengths in characters (code points); `title` 1-64 and `body` 1-240 characters after trim, no C0 control character or DEL except a newline in `body`; `link` up to 200 characters, one leading `/`, not `//`, no backslash or whitespace; usernames must have the username shape.
- **Errors:** 503 Debug is not configured; 401 Unauthorized; 400 `Expected a JSON body …`; 404 `{ error: 'No account with that username', usernames }` (nothing queued); 503 Push is not configured (send without VAPID).
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: GET /debug/push-broadcast/:id

- **Purpose:** Operator delivery report of one broadcast. Returns `{ id, queued, pending, delivered, dropped, failed }` from the push outbox rows that carry the broadcast id.
- **Counts:** `pending` is not finished yet (first try or retry); `delivered` is finished and reached at least one subscription (also when another subscription of the account failed for good); `dropped` is sent to none (the subscription was removed or gone before the send); `failed` gave up after the retry limit without reaching any subscription.
- **Errors:** 503 Debug is not configured; 401 Unauthorized; 404 Not found for an id that is not a UUID or that no broadcast row carries (rows of a forum or conversation message with that id do not count) (also a broadcast that queued nothing).
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: GET /debug/logins

- **Purpose:** Operator read of the login switch. Returns `{ open, source, defaultOpen, allowlist }`: `source` is `stored` once `PUT /debug/logins` ran and `default` before that (then `open` equals `defaultOpen` from `LOGINS_OPEN_DEFAULT`); `allowlist` is `[{ accountId, createdAt }]`, oldest first, `createdAt` ISO.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match.
- **Used by:** Operators before and after a release; `scripts/gifts-debug.sh logins`.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: PUT /debug/logins

- **Purpose:** Operator set of the login switch. Body `{ open: boolean }` (no other keys). Stores the value in `app_setting` (`logins_open`), which wins over `LOGINS_OPEN_DEFAULT` from then on, and returns the same JSON as `GET /debug/logins`. While closed, `POST /auth/passkey/register/begin`, `POST /auth/passkey/register/finish`, `POST /auth/passkey/authenticate/finish`, `POST /auth/passkey/recovery/begin`, and `POST /auth/passkey/recovery/finish` answer 503 `logins_closed` except for the platform account and allowlisted accounts (a new registration has no account yet and is always refused). Existing sessions are not touched. Logs `debug.logins.set`.
- **Errors:** 503 Debug is not configured; 401 Unauthorized; 400 `{ error: 'Expected a JSON body with an "open" boolean' }`.
- **Used by:** Operators closing logins for a release and opening them again; `scripts/gifts-debug.sh logins-open` / `logins-close`.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: POST /debug/logins/allowlist

- **Purpose:** Operator add of one account to the login allowlist. Body `{ accountId }` (UUID, any case; stored lower case). Returns `{ added, ...GET /debug/logins }`; `added` is `false` when the account was already listed (its first `createdAt` stays). Removing the account removes its row. Logs `debug.logins.allowlist.add`.
- **Errors:** 503 Debug is not configured; 401 Unauthorized; 400 `{ error: 'Expected a JSON body with an "accountId" uuid' }`; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` when no account has that id.
- **Used by:** Operators letting testers sign in while logins are closed; `scripts/gifts-debug.sh logins-allow`.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: DELETE /debug/logins/allowlist/:accountId

- **Purpose:** Operator removal of one account from the login allowlist. Returns `{ removed, ...GET /debug/logins }`; `removed` is `false` when the account was not listed. Does not end its sessions. Logs `debug.logins.allowlist.remove`.
- **Errors:** 503 Debug is not configured; 401 Unauthorized; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` when `:accountId` is not a UUID.
- **Used by:** Operators; `scripts/gifts-debug.sh logins-disallow`.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: DELETE /debug/sessions

- **Purpose:** Operator end of every member session: deletes every `auth_session` row and returns `{ deleted }` (row count). Every bearer that held one answers 401 from then on, so the app falls back to signed out; members sign in again (subject to the login switch). Operators authenticate with `DEBUG_TOKEN`, not a session, so no row is kept. Each deleted row is logged in `db_change` with the token hashed. Logs `debug.sessions.deleted` with the count.
- **Errors:** 503 Debug is not configured; 401 Unauthorized.
- **Used by:** Operators after a release that must sign every member out; `scripts/gifts-debug.sh end-sessions yes`.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: POST /debug/shop-notes

- **Purpose:** Stores one live top-level shop note that has a till account and no pin. Body `{ text, shopUsername, authorId }`. `text` must contain `#21GiftsShop`. The pin is not accepted and is stored null. Response `{ id, shopNpub }`. `POST /messages` still refuses a shop without a pin. The new key queues the place push and the photo push.
- **Errors:** 503 Debug is not configured; 401 Unauthorized; 503 Shop keys are not configured; 400 Invalid shop note; 404 Not found; 503 Shop key was not created; 503 Shop note was not stored.
- **Used by:** The directory test for a shop that already exists without a pin, and an operator recording that shape.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: POST /debug/shop-keys

- **Purpose:** Stores the OpenCryptoPay private key of one shop note that already has that key. Body `{ messageId, secret }`. `messageId` is the forum note. `secret` is 64 hex characters and is not logged or returned. Response `{ messageId, npub }`. The same key again is a success. A different key already stored is refused. The insert does not queue a place push. A public shop queues the photo push; any other note leaves the photo flags clear.
- **Errors:** 503 Debug is not configured; 503 Shop keys are not configured; 401 Unauthorized; 400 Invalid shop key; 404 Not found; 409 Shop key already exists.
- **Used by:** The operator import of shop keys that already exist at OpenCryptoPay.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: POST /debug/passkey-renew/reopen

- **Purpose:** Operator delete of one account's failed passkey-renew rows so the blocking dialog opens again at the explanation. Body `{ accountId }` UUID. Returns `{ deleted }`. Succeeded and cancelled rows stay. Does not change the account. Refuses when `walletRequired` is true.
- **Errors:** 503 Debug is not configured; 401 Unauthorized; 400 Invalid account; 404 Not found; 409 Account already has a seed.
- **Used by:** Operators who must ask one member to repeat passkey renew.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: POST /auth/passkey/authenticate/begin

- **Purpose:** Issues WebAuthn request options for a discoverable credential. JSON: challengeId, options (`extensions.prf.eval.first` = base64url SHA-256 of `21gifts-nostr-v1`).
- **Errors:** HTTP 500 `{ error: 'Server auth is not configured' }` if `WEBAUTHN_RP_ID` is unset, blank, not on the allowlist, or no CORS origin matches it. That 500 is logged as `auth.passkey.login.fail` with the same error and no account id. With `APP_BUILD_REQUIRED` set, a missing or different `X-App-Build` header is HTTP 403 `{ error: 'A new app version is ready. Reload the page.', code: 'app_outdated' }` before any other check (logged as `auth.app.outdated`). Not checked against the login switch: the account is unknown until finish.
- **Used by:** App passkey sign-in.
- **Auth:** Public.

## Endpoint: POST /auth/passkey/authenticate/finish

- **Purpose:** Verifies the assertion and issues `{ token, account }` immediately. Requires `Origin`. `{ token, account }` uses owner JSON including `hasPosted`, `aboutMe`, `aboutMeHasPhoto`, `notificationLevel`, `notifyHearts`, `amountUnit`, `funding`, `walletRequired`, `walletBackupSeenAt`, and `passkeyCredentialId`. An account with `sessionRefused` is refused with no bearer.
- **Errors:** 400 invalid body/origin/challenge/credential; 403 `{ error: 'You signed in with the wrong account. Please try again with the correct account.' }` when `sessionRefused` is true; 500 if WebAuthn is unconfigured. A missing body is 400 `Expected a JSON body with challengeId and credential`, logged as `auth.passkey.login.fail` with `json` `absent` and `bodyBytes`. Invalid JSON is 400 `Finish body is not valid JSON`, logged with `json` `invalid` and `bodyBytes`, and the text is not logged. A parsed body without `challengeId` and `credential` is the expected-body 400, logged with `json` `parsed`, `bodyKind`, and for an object `hasCredential` and `challengeIdKind`. The challenge id is logged only when it is 64 lowercase hex. The credential and the raw body are not logged. The 500 is logged as the same event with `Server auth is not configured`. With `APP_BUILD_REQUIRED` set, a missing or different `X-App-Build` header is HTTP 403 `{ error: 'A new app version is ready. Reload the page.', code: 'app_outdated' }` before any other check (logged as `auth.app.outdated`). While the operator login switch is closed (`GET /debug/logins`), HTTP 503 `{ error: 'Logins are paused for an update. Try again later.', code: 'logins_closed' }` after the assertion is verified and before the sign count or a session is written, unless the account is the platform account or on the login allowlist. A credential that is no stored passkey but the new passkey of a pending social recovery is HTTP 409 `{ error: 'Recovery is not finished', code: 'recovery_pending' }` instead of 400 `Unknown credential`, so the app keeps that passkey (the recovery may still commit with it). Without a pending recovery the credential is read once more, so a recovery commit that lands between the two reads signs in instead of answering `Unknown credential`.
- **Used by:** App passkey sign-in.
- **Auth:** Public (proof is the assertion).

## Endpoint: POST /auth/passkey/register/begin

- **Purpose:** Issues WebAuthn creation options. JSON: challengeId, options (`extensions.prf: {}`). Empty body, `{}`, or no `name` and no `viewKey` mints a pending new account id (row created only on finish); `user.name` is that UUID and `user.displayName` is `21.gifts`. Optional `{ "name": string }` with no `viewKey` normalizes with the username rules; on success `user.name` and `user.displayName` are that normalized string and `user.id` stays the new account UUID. Optional body `{ "viewKey": "<64-hex>" }` claims an operator-provisioned account (same id/name/username/viewKey) and ignores `name`.
- **Errors:** HTTP 500 `{ error: 'Server auth is not configured' }` if `WEBAUTHN_RP_ID` is unset, blank, not on the allowlist, or no CORS origin matches it; 400 `{ error: 'Expected a JSON body with an optional "viewKey" string' }` when `viewKey` is present but not a string; 400 `{ error: 'Expected a JSON body with an optional "name" string' }` when `name` is present and not a string; 400 `{ error: 'Username must be 1–32 characters of a-z, 0-9, hyphen, underscore, or dot' }` when `name` does not normalize; 404 `{ error: 'This profile could not be found.' }` for a malformed/unknown view key; 409 `{ error: 'This profile already has a passkey' }` when the provisioned account already has a credential; 409 `{ error: 'Username is already in use' }` when the normalized name is taken. Failed name checks write no challenge row. Invalid JSON is 400 `{ error: 'Begin body is not valid JSON' }`, logs `auth.passkey.register.fail` with `json` `invalid` and `bodyBytes`, and does not open a challenge. Those failures log `auth.passkey.register.fail` with the error text and not the view key. With `APP_BUILD_REQUIRED` set, a missing or different `X-App-Build` header is HTTP 403 `{ error: 'A new app version is ready. Reload the page.', code: 'app_outdated' }` before any other check (logged as `auth.app.outdated`). While the operator login switch is closed (`GET /debug/logins`), HTTP 503 `{ error: 'Logins are paused for an update. Try again later.', code: 'logins_closed' }` for a new registration (no body or `{ name }`), and for a claim (`{ viewKey }`) after the 404/409 checks unless the profile is the platform account or on the login allowlist.
- **Used by:** App passkey account creation and claim-by-viewKey.
- **Auth:** Public.

## Endpoint: POST /auth/passkey/register/finish

- **Purpose:** Verifies the attestation, creates a `linkingKey: null` account (or binds a passkey to a provisioned account without recreating it), issues `{ token, account }`. Requires `Origin`. When the challenge stored a name, the new account's `name` and `username` are that string. `{ token, account }` uses owner JSON including `hasPosted`, `aboutMe`, `aboutMeHasPhoto`, `notificationLevel`, `notifyHearts`, `amountUnit`, `funding`, `walletRequired`, `walletBackupSeenAt`, and `passkeyCredentialId`. An account with `sessionRefused` is refused with no bearer.
- **Errors:** 400 invalid body/origin/challenge/passkey; 400 `{ error: 'Username must be 1–32 characters of a-z, 0-9, hyphen, underscore, or dot' }` when the stored name no longer normalizes; 403 `{ error: 'You signed in with the wrong account. Please try again with the correct account.' }` when `sessionRefused` is true; 409 `{ error: 'Username is already in use' }` when that handle is taken or the account row was not inserted. Those name failures create no credential and no session. 500 if WebAuthn is unconfigured. A missing body is 400 `Expected a JSON body with challengeId and credential`, logged as `auth.passkey.register.fail` with `json` `absent` and `bodyBytes`. Invalid JSON is 400 `Finish body is not valid JSON`, logged with `json` `invalid` and `bodyBytes`, and the text is not logged. A parsed body without `challengeId` and `credential` is the expected-body 400, logged with `json` `parsed`, `bodyKind`, and for an object `hasCredential` and `challengeIdKind`. The challenge id is logged only when it is 64 lowercase hex. The credential and the raw body are not logged. The 500 is logged as the same event with `Server auth is not configured`. With `APP_BUILD_REQUIRED` set, a missing or different `X-App-Build` header is HTTP 403 `{ error: 'A new app version is ready. Reload the page.', code: 'app_outdated' }` before any other check (logged as `auth.app.outdated`). While the operator login switch is closed (`GET /debug/logins`), HTTP 503 `{ error: 'Logins are paused for an update. Try again later.', code: 'logins_closed' }` after the attestation is verified and before any account, credential, or session write; a new account is always refused, a claimed profile only when it is neither the platform account nor on the login allowlist.
- **Used by:** App passkey account creation and claim-by-viewKey.
- **Auth:** Public (proof is the attestation).

## Endpoint: POST /auth/passkey/recovery/begin

- **Purpose:** Starts a social recovery (docs/social-recovery.md). Body `{ "sparkPubkey": "<old wallet identity key>", "lookup": { "signature": "<lowercase hex DER>", "timestamp": <Unix seconds> } }` (strict). The api forwards the old wallet's signed lookup to the LNURL server (`POST lnurlpay/<key>/recover`) and, when that lookup is verified and the key still holds the account's username, answers 200 `{ challengeId, options, accountId, username }`: WebAuthn creation options as for a seed passkey (user id and name the account id, no `excludeCredentials`) and a stored `recovery` challenge. Earlier recovery challenges of the account are consumed and an unforwarded pending recovery is dropped. A forwarded pending recovery is settled first with this lookup (commit, drop, or keep after the 30 s window). Never logs the key, the signature, or the timestamp.
- **Errors:** HTTP 403 `{ error: 'A new app version is ready. Reload the page.', code: 'app_outdated' }` first when `APP_BUILD_REQUIRED` is set and `X-App-Build` is missing or different; 500 `{ error: 'Server auth is not configured' }`; 503 `{ error: 'Lightning address service is unavailable' }` when the LNURL server is off; 429 `{ error: 'Too many requests' }` (30 a minute per address); 400 `{ error: 'Begin body is not valid JSON' }`; 400 `{ error: 'Expected a JSON body with sparkPubkey and lookup' }`; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` when no account and no previous wallet has the key; 409 `{ error: 'This account was already recovered. Sign in with the new passkey.', code: 'recovery_done' }` for a previous wallet's key, a forwarded recovery this lookup commits, or a commit that landed while the lookup ran (no account holds the key verified any more and a previous wallet has it); 409 `{ error: 'This account needs an operator to recover it', code: 'recovery_operator' }` when the old key no longer holds the username and is not a previous wallet by then; 403 `{ error: 'You signed in with the wrong account. Please try again with the correct account.' }` for a session-refused account; 503 `{ error: 'Logins are paused for an update. Try again later.', code: 'logins_closed' }` while the login switch is closed (unless the platform account or allowlisted); 503 `{ error: 'Lightning address service is unavailable' }` while the lookup is not verified, a forwarded recovery is kept, a settling commit does not land, or the store fails. Each refusal except 429 and the build-check 403 (logged as `auth.app.outdated`) logs `auth.passkey.recovery.fail` with `stage: 'begin'`; a lookup forward that does not reach the LNURL server (including the 15 s abort) also logs `lnurl_server.unreachable` with `route: 'recovery'`.
- **Used by:** App `/recover` (signed-out recovery with the old 12 words).
- **Auth:** Public (proof is the old wallet's signed lookup, verified by the LNURL server).

## Endpoint: POST /auth/passkey/recovery/finish

- **Purpose:** Stores a pending social recovery. Requires `Origin`. Body `{ "challengeId", "credential": <attestation without PRF results>, "newSparkPubkey": "<new wallet identity key>", "signature": { "pubkey": "<old key>", "signature": "<128 hex r||s>" } }` (strict). Verifies the new passkey's attestation against the `recovery` challenge and the old wallet's compact low-S signature over the five-line recovery message (with the credential id from the verified attestation), then consumes the challenge and stores the pending recovery in one locked step. Answers 200 `{ "status": "pending" }` and mints no session; the account changes only when the address transfer commits. A repeat of the finish that stored the pending recovery, with the same challenge, credential id and new wallet key, answers 200 again and changes nothing; any other use of a consumed challenge is refused (`Challenge already used`).
- **Errors:** 403 `app_outdated` first (as begin); 500 `Server auth is not configured`; 429 `Too many requests` (30 a minute per address); 400 `Expected a JSON body with challengeId, credential, newSparkPubkey and signature` (absent body, wrong shape, missing credential, or a key that does not normalize), `Finish body is not valid JSON`, `Invalid origin`, `Unknown or expired challenge`, `Challenge expired`, `Challenge already used`, `Wrong challenge type`, `Invalid passkey`, `Invalid recovery signature`; 403 wrong account for a session-refused account; 503 `logins_closed` while the login switch is closed; 409 `{ error: 'A recovery is already pending' }` and `{ error: 'Wallet key is already in use' }`; 503 `{ error: 'Lightning address service is unavailable' }` when the store fails (including a unique violation). Each refusal except 429 and the build-check 403 (logged as `auth.app.outdated`) logs `auth.passkey.recovery.fail` with `stage: 'finish'`, the account id once known, and the challenge id only when it is 64 lowercase hex; never the keys, the signature, the credential, or the body.
- **Used by:** App `/recover`, after the new passkey and wallet exist.
- **Auth:** Public (proof is the attestation and the old wallet's signature).

## Endpoint: POST /auth/passkey/replace/begin

- **Purpose:** Bearer session. Refuses to replace a passkey. A recovery phrase is never replaced here; only a social recovery commit (after `POST /auth/passkey/recovery/finish`, through `POST /lnurlpay/:pubkey/transfer` or a settling lookup) gives an account a different one. Does not create a challenge and does not delete or insert a credential. `walletBackupSeenAt` is not consulted.
- **Errors:** 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` missing or invalid Bearer; 409 `{ error: 'A recovery phrase cannot be replaced' }` after a valid session; 500 `{ error: 'Server auth is not configured' }` when WebAuthn is unconfigured (checked before the bearer). That 500 is logged as `auth.passkey.replace.refused` with the same error and no account id.
- **Used by:** App passkey replace, which the api now refuses.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session.

## Endpoint: POST /auth/passkey/replace/finish

- **Purpose:** Bearer session. Same refusal as begin. Does not parse a ceremony once the session is valid. Does not delete or insert a passkey. Does not mint a session. `walletBackupSeenAt` does not decide whether a seed exists.
- **Errors:** 401 without a session; 409 `{ error: 'A recovery phrase cannot be replaced' }` after a valid session; 500 if WebAuthn is unconfigured. That 500 is logged as `auth.passkey.replace.refused` with `Server auth is not configured` and no account id.
- **Used by:** App passkey replace, which the api now refuses.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session.

## Endpoint: POST /auth/passkey/seed/begin

- **Purpose:** Bearer session. When `walletRequired` is not true, issues WebAuthn creation options for one extra seed passkey (`{ challengeId, options }`, no `excludeCredentials`, user id and name are the account id). Does not delete the login passkey. `walletRequired: true` means a seed passkey already exists. A 409 because a seed already exists stores a failed renew row (`stage` `begin`, HTTP 409), logs `auth.passkey.seed.fail` with the account id and error, and does not change the account. A 200 stores no renew row. 401 stores no row and no diagnostic row. 500 stores no renew row and logs `auth.passkey.seed.fail` with `Server auth is not configured`.
- **Errors:** 401 missing or invalid Bearer; 409 `{ error: 'This account already has a recovery phrase' }` when `walletRequired` is true (no challenge); 500 if WebAuthn is unconfigured.
- **Used by:** App add-recovery-phrase for an account that has no seed yet.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session.

## Endpoint: POST /auth/passkey/seed/finish

- **Purpose:** Bearer session. Verifies a `seed` attestation and inserts an additional passkey, setting `walletRequired` true. Does not delete the login passkey, does not change `walletBackupSeenAt`, and does not mint a session. Success JSON is the owner account itself, same shape as `GET /me`, with no `token` and no `account` wrapper. `passkeyCredentialId` is the new credential id. `walletBackupSeenAt` does not decide whether a seed exists. A 400 or 409 after the session is known stores a failed renew row and does not change the account. Success stores `outcome` `succeeded` with null error fields, then acknowledges failed rows that are still unacknowledged, so the owner JSON has `walletRequired` true and `passkeyRenewClosed` false. 401 stores no renew row and no diagnostic row. 500 stores no renew row and logs `auth.passkey.seed.fail` with `Server auth is not configured`. A missing finish body is 400 `Expected a JSON body with challengeId and credential`, logged as `auth.passkey.seed.fail` with the account id, `json` `absent`, and `bodyBytes`. Invalid JSON is 400 `Finish body is not valid JSON`, logged with the account id, `json` `invalid`, and `bodyBytes`; the text is not logged. A parsed body without `challengeId` and `credential` is the expected-body 400, logged with the account id, `json` `parsed`, `bodyKind`, and for an object `hasCredential` and `challengeIdKind`. The challenge id is logged only when it is 64 lowercase hex. Each of those 400s still stores the failed renew row. The credential is not logged. A later ceremony 400 or 409 also logs `auth.passkey.seed.fail` with the account id.
- **Errors:** 401 without a session, including a `sessionRefused` bearer (resolved before finish, not 409); 409 `{ error: 'This account already has a recovery phrase' }` when `walletRequired` is already true, the credential id is taken, the account is missing, or the insert does not land; 400 invalid body, origin, challenge, or attestation; 500 if WebAuthn is unconfigured. Invalid JSON (`Finish body is not valid JSON`) and a body without `challengeId` and `credential` log `auth.passkey.seed.fail` with the account id as well as the renew row. The text, credential, and bearer are not logged. The 500 logs that fail event and stores no renew row.
- **Used by:** App add-recovery-phrase finish.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session.

## Endpoint: GET /favicon.ico

- **Purpose:** Windows ICO (RGBA PNG-in-ICO) of the 21.gifts mark. `Content-Type: image/x-icon`, `Cache-Control: public, max-age=86400`.
- **Errors:** 404 empty body when `public/favicon.ico` is missing.
- **Used by:** Browsers opening api.21.gifts.
- **Auth:** See Purpose — Bearer where stated, else public.

## Endpoint: GET /favicon.svg

- **Purpose:** SVG mark, orange 21 on black. `Content-Type: image/svg+xml`, `Cache-Control: public, max-age=86400`.
- **Errors:** 404 empty body when `public/favicon.svg` is missing.
- **Used by:** Modern browsers preferring SVG icons.
- **Auth:** See Purpose — Bearer where stated, else public.

## Endpoint: GET /gifts

- **Purpose:** Public JSON of outbound gifts for one UTC day (`?day=YYYY-MM-DD`): `giftCount`, totals (`totalSats` / `totalBtc` / `totalUsd` plus additive `totalChf` / `totalEur` / `totalPhp`), `gifts[]` (`paidAt`, `amountSats`, `amountBtc`, `amountUsd`, `amountChf` / `amountEur` / `amountPhp`, `recipient`), and `fx` (`quote` stays BTC-USD; `fx.quotes` is USD only). Each currency is that gift's stored string. A missing string stays null. It is not filled from another gift, from satoshis, or from a daily close. The day's sum for a currency adds only the gifts that stored that string, and is null only when every gift that day lacks it. One null does not null the other currencies. Empty day is 200 with zeros and USD-only `fx.quotes` (no Coinbase / Frankfurter). No invoices.
- **Errors:** 400 `{ "error": "Expected a UTC day (YYYY-MM-DD)" }` when `day` is missing or not a real date; 503 `{ "error": "Gift stats are unavailable" }` only when the gift store throws (`gifts.day.failed`). A missing stored amount is JSON null and 200.
- **Used by:** App day page (`GET /gifts` same-origin proxy).
- **Auth:** Public.

## Endpoint: GET /me/activity

- **Purpose:** Bearer session JSON of given and received sats for the signed-in account (`donatedSats`, `receivedSats`, `donatedOverTime`, `receivedOverTime`, `owedSats`, `creditSats`, `owedOverTime`, `creditOverTime`, `fx`). Given is confirmed forum zaps this account paid on notes that are not loans, excluding repay: repayment invoices, plus outbound house gifts of kind daily and welcome when the account is platform. Moderator stipends (kind === 'moderator') are omitted. Received is zaps on authored notes that are not loans (including hidden notes and replies) plus `message.sats` remainder on top-level non-loan notes only (a loan note is omitted entirely, including a hidden one; gift-as-reply sats are not Received) plus house gifts of kind daily and welcome to the Lightning handle. A loan is a note with `goalRepayable === true`. Loans stay out of donated and received. `owedSats` is what this account still owes; `creditSats` is what others still owe this account; loan series are SpendDay rows only on days with a movement, with sats and BTC and every fiat field null. Donation series are the same `spendOverTime` day objects as `GET /gifts/stats` including additive CHF/EUR/PHP and `officialCount`. Zap rows and `message.sats` remainders are not kind `daily` or `welcome`, so they do not increment `officialCount`. Stored house gifts do, by their kind. Each currency is its own stored string. A missing string stays null and is not scaled from another gift or from a daily close. One null does not null the other currencies, that day's other sums, or a later cumulative. Empty activity is 200 zeros with USD-only `fx.quotes` (no Coinbase / Frankfurter). Missing CHF/EUR/PHP is JSON `null`. No invoices or payment hashes.
- **Errors:** 401 `{ "error": "Unauthorized" }` without session; 503 `{ "error": "Gift stats are unavailable" }` when the activity build throws. The log is `account.activity.fx_incomplete` when the thrown error message is exactly `fx.rate.missing`, and `account.activity.failed` otherwise. A missing stored amount is JSON null and 200.
- **Used by:** App `/me/activity` proxy, signed-in profile chart and menu totals.
- **Auth:** Bearer session. No living-room-rules gate.

## Endpoint: GET /me/loans

- **Purpose:** The signed-in member's own loans in one call. Bearer session. 200 `{ sundayRest, loans, lenderWaiting }`. `loans` lists the member's live repayable asks that are still collecting or not fully repaid, newest first; fully repaid loans are left out, as are hidden notes. Each loan has `messageId`, `text` (first 160 characters of the note), `createdAt`, `goalSats`, `sats`, `goalCurrency`, `goalAmount`, `goalAmountUsd` / `goalAmountChf` / `goalAmountEur` / `goalAmountPhp`, `amountUsd` / `amountChf` / `amountEur` / `amountPhp`, `termDays`, `fundedAt` (null while collecting), `daysDue`, `daysPaid`, `repaidSats`, `totalSats`, `due`, and `next`.
- **Due and next:** `due` covers every due, unpaid share: `payableSats`, `payableAmount`, and `payablePeople` for lenders who can receive; `waitingSats`, `waitingAmount`, `waitingPeople`, and `waitingShares` for lenders who cannot; `behindDays` (due days before today that still have an unpaid share that is not waiting and did not fall due before its lender's wallet was verified, as `daysPaid` of `GET /messages/:id/repayment`); `lastPayment` (paying `payable` would finish the loan). `next` is the next repayment day after today `{ dueOn, sats, amount }`, or null. Amounts are two-decimal strings in the loan's currency for a fiat loan and null for bitcoin. For a fiat loan every sat figure that is not yet paid (`totalSats`, `payableSats`, `waitingSats`, `next.sats`) is priced at the rate `POST /messages/:id/repayment` would use now, read once per request for every loan, and is null without a rate. `sundayRest` is true when the device `Time-Zone` is in Sunday, the rule that refuses repayments today.
- **Lender waiting:** `lenderWaiting` `{ shares, loans, sats }` is the caller's side as a lender: the due, unpaid shares owed to the caller while the caller cannot receive (no verified wallet), over every live funded loan the caller gave to: found through the caller's in-app invoices and counted only when the caller is one of its givers. `shares` counts them, `loans` the loans they belong to, and `sats` is their sum in sats (a fiat share priced like `waitingSats`; null when one has no rate). All zero when the caller can receive. The app shows it as a card that asks the lender to set up the wallet.
- **Errors:** 401 Unauthorized; 503 `{ error: 'Ask amount is unavailable' }` when the debt of a loan of the caller, or of a loan the caller gave to, cannot be computed, or the rate cannot be read; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` when the store fails.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session. Read-only; not refused on Sunday.
- **Used by:** The loans card, the menu row, and the repayment screen.

## Endpoint: GET /members/:accountId/activity

- **Purpose:** Same activity JSON as `GET /me/activity` for the member `:accountId`. A session has the same auth as `GET /members/:accountId`; an operator token skips the session and `forum.read` gate.
- **Errors:** 401 without a session or operator token; 409 `missing_requirements` when the session caller lacks rules (session path only); 404 non-uuid or unknown account; 503 `{ "error": "Gift stats are unavailable" }` when the activity build throws. Missing fiat is never 503 (same as `GET /me/activity`).
- **Used by:** App `/forum/members/[accountId]/activity` proxy and member profile chart.
- **Auth:** Bearer session with `forum.read`. The operator `DEBUG_TOKEN` or `DEBUG_READ_TOKEN` reads the same body without a session (`isOperatorBearer`); a bearer that matches neither falls through to this session check.

## Endpoint: GET /view/:viewKey/activity

- **Purpose:** Public activity JSON for the account behind the 64-hex view key. Same body as `GET /me/activity`.
- **Errors:** 404 `{ "error": "Not found" }` for a bad or unknown key (same as `GET /view/:viewKey`); 503 `{ "error": "Gift stats are unavailable" }` when the activity build throws. Missing fiat is never 503 (same as `GET /me/activity`).
- **Used by:** App `/view-key/[viewKey]/activity` proxy and public view profile chart.
- **Auth:** none.

## Endpoint: GET /messages/stats

- **Purpose:** Public count of living forum notes and replies together. `postCount` is the total. `postsOverTime` is one row per UTC day from the first living note through today (or a later note), with `postCount: 0` on days that had none. Soft-hidden notes are omitted. A reply counts the same as a top-level note.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ "error": "Post stats are unavailable" }` when the count query throws (`posts.stats.failed`). An empty forum is 200 `{ postCount: 0, postsOverTime: [] }`.
- **Used by:** The public statistics page.
- **Auth:** Public.

## Endpoint: GET /fx/spot

- **Purpose:** Public current price of 1 BTC in USD, CHF, EUR, and PHP, independent of gifts: `{ asOf, source, rates }` where `asOf` is the ISO fetch time, `source` is `coinbase-exchange-rates`, and `rates` holds fiat per 1 BTC as decimal text at provider precision. A currency without a usable quote is omitted from `rates`. The background worker refreshes the stored quote every 5 minutes from one Coinbase response (`BTC_FIAT_SPOT_URL`); the request never calls the provider. A failed fetch keeps the last good quote and its `asOf` (Postgres `btc_fiat_spot` survives a restart). Does not change `GET /gifts/stats`.
- **Errors:** None. No quote ever fetched, or a store that throws (`fx.spot.read_failed`), is 200 `{ asOf: null, source: null, rates: {} }` with `Cache-Control: no-store`; a stored quote is cached `public, max-age=60`.
- **Used by:** App fiat amount entry (point of sale, gift amounts).
- **Auth:** Public.

## Endpoint: GET /gifts/stats

- **Purpose:** Public JSON of outbound gift totals: `totalSats` / `totalBtc` / `totalUsd` plus additive `totalChf` / `totalEur` / `totalPhp`, `giftCount`, `recipientCount`, date range, `spendOverTime` (giftCount+officialCount+sats+BTC+USD+fiat; `officialCount` is the distinct case-insensitive recipient handles that UTC day with kind `daily` or `welcome`, one person once, moderator excluded, gap days 0; `giftCount` remains every outbound row), `byRecipient`, `byMonth`, and `fx` (`quote` stays BTC-USD; `fx.quotes` is USD only). Each currency is its own stored string. A missing string stays null and is not filled from another gift, from satoshis, or from a daily close. A sum adds only the rows that stored that string, and is null only when every row in that sum lacks it. One null does not null the other currencies or a later cumulative. Optional query `recipient` filters to one recipient handle (the local part of the receiving address; case-insensitive). When `recipient` contains `@` after the first character, the local-part before `@` is used; otherwise the whole trimmed string. Missing/blank `recipient` = unfiltered. Unknown handle = empty stats **200** with zeros and USD-only `fx.quotes` (no Coinbase / Frankfurter). Empty boots are empty **200** with zeros and USD-only `fx.quotes` (no Coinbase / Frankfurter). No invoices.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ "error": "Gift stats are unavailable" }` only when the gift store throws (`gifts.stats.failed`). A missing stored amount is JSON null and 200.
- **Used by:** App statistics page (`GET /gifts/stats` same-origin proxy); optional per-recipient view via `?recipient=`; staff payout-goal widget on `/moderate`; the habit tracker pay sheet when a session is present, for the same fiat suffix as a forum zap.
- **Auth:** Public.

## Endpoint: GET /healthz

- **Purpose:** Liveness. `{ status: 'ok', service, version, wallet }`. `wallet` is `{ lnurlServer, zapSigning, freePayments, passkey }`, booleans fixed at boot (see `walletFeatures`); it never carries an env value, key, or URL and never changes the status. Not logged as http.request.
- **Errors:** Always 200 if the process is up.
- **Used by:** Orchestrators, e2e, Uptime checks.
- **Auth:** See Purpose — Bearer where stated, else public.

## Endpoint: GET /info

- **Purpose:** Service metadata (name, version, description, repo).
- **Errors:** 200 JSON.
- **Used by:** Humans and service catalogs.
- **Auth:** See Purpose — Bearer where stated, else public.

## Endpoint: GET /invoices/passkey

- **Purpose:** Spend-worker only. Query `address=local@domain`. Returns `{ hasPasskey: boolean }` so spend can filter before preflight. `address` is matched only as the wallet-backed `<username>@<host of PUBLIC_BASE_URL>` (trimmed, case-insensitive): the username is looked up and the account must have a verified wallet (`accountByReceivingAddress`). Another domain, a member without a verified wallet, or the LNURL server being off is the unknown-address answer. Fail closed: unknown address or account without a passkey credential → `hasPasskey: false` (always HTTP 200 on success; never 404).
- **Errors:** 503 if the token env is unset; 401 wrong/missing Bearer; 400 missing or invalid Lightning Address (`Not a valid Lightning Address (expected name@domain)`; an address on the configured wallet host passes even when that host has a port or is an IP address).
- **Used by:** the external spend worker before issuing a gift invoice.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` matching `SPEND_API_TOKEN`.

## Endpoint: GET /invoices/eligible

- **Purpose:** Spend-worker only. Query `address=local@domain`. `address` is matched only as the wallet-backed `<username>@<host of PUBLIC_BASE_URL>` (trimmed, case-insensitive): the username is looked up and the account must have a verified wallet (`accountByReceivingAddress`). Another domain, a member without a verified wallet, or the LNURL server being off is the unknown-address answer. Returns `{ eligible: boolean, status }` so spend can filter before issue and pay admitted members without a roster entry (1 USD). `status` is `effectiveStatus` (`'none' | 'pending' | 'trial' | 'admitted' | 'rejected'`). Unknown address and `basis` are always `{ eligible: false, status: 'none' }` (`basis` must not leak a stored grant). Before UTC `2026-10-01` (`FUNDING_REQUIRED_FROM_UTC`), every other role is `eligible: true` without a grant (`status` still follows `effectiveStatus`). From that day, fail closed unless admitted or trial-today **and** a 21.gifts recovery share is stored for the member's current verified wallet (`loadPayoutEligibility`; always HTTP 200 on success; never 404). A member in the program without that share is `{ eligible: false, status: 'admitted' }` (or `'trial'`): `status` stays the true grant status. Expired trial is `status: 'pending'` and `eligible: false` on the gate day.
- **Errors:** 503 if the token env is unset; 401 wrong/missing Bearer; 400 missing or invalid Lightning Address (`Not a valid Lightning Address (expected name@domain)`; an address on the configured wallet host passes even when that host has a port or is an IP address).
- **Used by:** the external spend worker before issuing a gift invoice.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` matching `SPEND_API_TOKEN`.

## Endpoint: GET /invoices/posted

- **Purpose:** Spend-worker only. Query `address=local@domain`. `address` is matched only as the wallet-backed `<username>@<host of PUBLIC_BASE_URL>` (trimmed, case-insensitive): the username is looked up and the account must have a verified wallet (`accountByReceivingAddress`). Another domain, a member without a verified wallet, or the LNURL server being off is the unknown-address answer. Returns `{ hasPosted, messageId, postedAt, hasMedia, welcomeHasMedia, welcomeMessageId }` (`messageId` newest live top-level non-profile id, or null; `postedAt` that row's `createdAt` ISO-8601, or null). `hasPosted` is still any live top-level non-profile post (no media requirement). An account that already received the welcome gift (a platform `Welcome` reply under one of its notes, live or hidden, a recorded `welcome` gift paid to its legacy external address, or a recorded `welcome` gift with description `21gifts welcome` under its username at or after its wallet verification) reports `welcomeHasMedia: false` and `welcomeMessageId: null`. `hasMedia` is true only when such a post has photo 0, extra stills, or video. `welcomeHasMedia` is true when any live top-level photo or video exists, including the About-me note. `welcomeMessageId` is that newest note's id, or null. Daily `hasPosted` / `hasMedia` still exclude the About-me note. `hasPosted: false` always pairs with `messageId: null`, `postedAt: null`, and `hasMedia: false`. `hasPosted: true` can still have `messageId: null` and `postedAt: null` when `listPostsByAccount` yields no non-profile row; `hasMedia` can still be true from an older media post when the newest listed row is text-only. Fail closed: unknown address, or account with no live **top-level** forum message that is not the auto-created profile note → `hasPosted: false`, `messageId: null`, `postedAt: null`, `hasMedia: false` (`welcomeHasMedia` can still be true when About me has a photo or video) (always HTTP 200 on success; never 404). Replies do not count. Photo-only / empty-text top-level notes still count for `hasPosted`. Damus-only rows (`accountId` null) and soft-deleted rows do not.
- **Errors:** 503 if the token env is unset; 401 wrong/missing Bearer; 400 missing or invalid Lightning Address (`Not a valid Lightning Address (expected name@domain)`; an address on the configured wallet host passes even when that host has a port or is an IP address).
- **Used by:** the external spend worker before issuing a gift invoice.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` matching `SPEND_API_TOKEN`.

## Endpoint: POST /invoices

- **Purpose:** Spend-worker only. Bearer `SPEND_API_TOKEN`. Body `{ address, amountMsat, amountUsd?, comment?, messageId?, groupMessageId? }` (`comment` max 255). Optional `amountUsd` is a positive amount with at most two decimals and at most 100000; a present value is stored as that two-decimal USD (`"5"` becomes `"5.00"`), and a bad present value is the same 400 as a bad body. An absent key stays unset. Optional `messageId` UUID; when set, that note must be that author's live top-level note, including About me, **and** have a photo or video (else 403 Forum post required; a text-only profile note stays 403); 503 `Platform account is not configured` if no isPlatform account. Omitted `messageId` stays any live top-level non-profile post (no media requirement; moderator stipend). Optional `groupMessageId` UUID, mutually exclusive with `messageId`. Display-only: stored only when it is that address's message in the closed `moderator_group` thread and an `isPlatform` account exists; otherwise it is not stored, `invoice.group_message_ignored` is logged, and the welcome-paid refusal still applies. A missing conversation store never blocks the 200. The living-room post gate still applies when `groupMessageId` is set. Requires a 21.gifts member whose wallet-backed address is `address` (`<username>@<host of PUBLIC_BASE_URL>`, case-insensitive, verified wallet; a member there with a passkey but no verified wallet is 403 Wallet required; any other domain or no such member is 403 Passkey required) that already has a passkey credential, passes the payout gate (`loadPayoutEligibility`: before UTC `2026-10-01` every non-`basis` role without a grant; from that day admitted, or trial whose `trialUtcDate` is today UTC, and a 21.gifts recovery share stored for the current verified wallet; `basis` never), When `messageId` is omitted, it also requires at least one live **top-level** forum message that is not the auto-created profile note. Replies do not unlock (`Forum post required`). After that gate, a request whose `comment` is not exactly `Welcome` and that does not store a resolved moderator `groupMessageId` lists outbound gifts when a gift store is configured. A list throw is 503 `Gift ledger unreadable` and does not fetch an invoice. A welcome gift for the same handle on the current UTC day is 403 `Welcome gift already paid`. Comment exactly `Welcome`, a stored moderator `groupMessageId`, or an omitted gift store skips that refusal. Then fetches a BOLT11 for the canonical lower-case wallet address through the internal LNURL-server resolver (`lnurlServerFetch`; never over the public URL), decodes hash/amount, and stores the invoice in memory under that address.
- **Errors:** 503 if the token env is unset; 503 `{ error: 'Platform account is not configured' }` when `messageId` is set and there is no `isPlatform` account (before LNURL); 409 `{ error: 'Welcome gift already paid' }` when `comment` is `Welcome` (with or without `messageId`; not for a resolved moderator `groupMessageId`, which is recorded as `moderator`) and the account already has a platform `Welcome` reply under one of its notes, a recorded `welcome` gift paid to its legacy external address, or a recorded `welcome` gift with description `21gifts welcome` under its username at or after its wallet verification (after the passkey and grant checks, before LNURL; the welcome gift is once per account); 401 wrong/missing Bearer; 400 bad JSON/address (an address on the configured wallet host passes even when that host has a port or is an IP address)/amount/`comment` longer than 255, invalid `messageId` or `groupMessageId` UUID, or both `messageId` and `groupMessageId` set; 403 `{ error: 'Wallet required' }` when `address` is the wallet-backed address of a member who has a passkey but no verified wallet (before LNURL; logs `invoice.wallet_required`); 403 `{ error: 'Passkey required' }` for any other address that finds no member with a verified wallet (another domain, an unknown username, the LNURL server off, or a member with neither a passkey nor a verified wallet) or a member with a verified wallet but no passkey (before LNURL); 403 `{ error: 'Funding grant required' }` when the account has a passkey but fails the payout gate (after passkey, before the living-room post check and LNURL; grant required from UTC 2026-10-01, and with it the recovery share; a member in the program without the share gets the same 403 and the same log `invoice.funding_required`, and so do the welcome gift and the moderator stipend); 403 `{ error: 'Forum post required' }` when `messageId` is omitted and the account has a passkey and is eligible but no live top-level non-profile forum row, or when `messageId` is set but is not that author's live top-level note (About me included) with a photo or video (after grant, before LNURL); 503 `{ error: 'Gift ledger unreadable' }` when listing outbound gifts throws (after the forum gate, before LNURL; only when `comment` is not exactly `Welcome`, no moderator `groupMessageId` is stored, and a gift store is configured); 403 `{ error: 'Welcome gift already paid' }` when that list shows a welcome gift for the same handle on the current UTC day (same exemptions); 502 provider did not issue a matching invoice.
- **Used by:** the external spend worker before paying via lightning.space.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` matching `SPEND_API_TOKEN`.

## Endpoint: POST /invoices/proof

- **Purpose:** Spend-worker only. Body `{ id, preimage }`. Accepts the payment preimage as proof (`sha256(preimage)` must equal the stored payment hash). Idempotent for the same preimage. A match inserts an outbound `gift` row when `DATABASE_URL` is set (BOLT11 `pr` as `lightning_invoice`, amount floor(msat/1000) sats, fee 0, recipient handle from the address, description `21gifts moderator` when the invoice has `groupMessageId`, else `21gifts welcome` when `comment` is exactly `Welcome`, else `21gifts daily`, `kind` `moderator` when `groupMessageId` is set else `welcome` when `comment` is exactly `Welcome` else `daily` (older welcome gifts kept description `21gifts daily`; they count for the welcome checks only when paid to the account's legacy external address), `source_wallet` `lightning.space`); otherwise recording is a no-op. Insert failure logs `gifts.record_failed` and still returns 200. When `invoice.messageId` is set, inserts a platform gift-reply first, then `addSats` (idempotent). Platform gift-replies do not notify (no in-app rows, no Web Push; `messages.reply.notify.failed` is not logged on this path; the nested gift-reply still persists); when that message is already a reply (`parentId` set), persists a deterministic `spendGiftReplyId` marker under that reply, `markDeleted` so live `listReplies` omits it, then `addReceivedSats`s the reply (a live existing marker is `markDeleted` only and does not `addReceivedSats`; no `notifyForumReply`); skip and log `invoice.gift_reply.failed` if parent/platform missing; still 200. When `invoice.groupMessageId` is set, inserts one platform conversation message in that closed `moderator_group` thread (name trimmed or `21.gifts`, `sats` = floor(msat/1000), text = comment · recipient name, `giftForMessageId` set to the triggering message's id); skip and log `invoice.group_gift.failed` if the triggering row/thread/platform is missing; still 200. Repeat proof is idempotent on the deterministic stipend id. Proof stores a supplied amountUsd as that USD string and stores CHF, EUR, and PHP null. It does not read the daily rate book. An omitted amountUsd stores null for all four. A string that is not a normalized amount stores null.
- **Errors:** 503 unconfigured; 401 unauthorized; 400 bad body or hash mismatch on an unexpired invoice; 404 unknown id (including after unpaid sweep/restart); 409 expired without a matching preimage, or already paid with a different preimage. Matching preimage is 200 after TTL while the row remains.
- **Used by:** the external spend worker after LNDHub `payinvoice` returns a preimage.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` matching `SPEND_API_TOKEN`.

## Endpoint: POST /spend/daily-instruction

- **Purpose:** Spend-worker only. Bearer `SPEND_API_TOKEN`. Body `{ address }`. Extra keys are ignored. The kind is always daily. Auth runs before JSON parsing and before gifts or roster. The address is matched as a member's receiving address (`<username>@<host>` with a verified wallet); any other address is a missing account. After a valid address, facts are: missing account → no passkey, no post, no media, `messageId` null, not eligible, omit grant status (no message or funding read); else passkey, `hasPosted` and `hasMedia` as `GET /invoices/posted` (no media lookup when `hasPosted` is false), `messageId` the newest listed live top-level non-profile post with a photo, a video, or `photoCount` > 0 (null when that capped list has none; a text-only newest id is not sent), and eligible / grant status as `GET /invoices/eligible` (`basis` omits the grant). A configured gift store lists outbound gifts and sets the same-UTC-day welcome flag; an omitted gift store does not apply that rule. Then the daily roster is read and `decideCliDailyInstruction` returns the skip or pay; a stored roster row counts only for the account that receives on `address`. A skip is not logged. The token, comments, and Lightning addresses are not logged.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Spend invoices are not configured' }` if the token env is unset (before body and roster); 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` wrong/missing Bearer; 400 `{ error: 'Expected a JSON body with address' }` on JSON parse failure, a non-object body, or a missing or non-string `address`; 400 `{ error: 'Not a valid Lightning Address (expected name@domain)' }` when `address` is a string that is not a Lightning Address; 503 `{ error: 'Gift ledger unreadable' }` when listing outbound gifts throws (roster `get` is not called); a `DailyRosterRequestError` keeps its status and `{ error }`; any other roster throw is 502 `{ error: 'Daily roster is unavailable' }`.
- **Used by:** the external spend worker before paying a daily gift.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` matching `SPEND_API_TOKEN`.

## Endpoint: GET /lightning-address

- **Purpose:** Query `address=local@domain`. Resolves LUD-16, cached 5 minutes on success. For a member's wallet address (`<username>@<host of PUBLIC_BASE_URL>`) the returned callback is the api's own `/lnurlp/:username/invoice`, forwarded to the self-hosted LNURL server (the api sees the amount and the invoice, never pays or holds funds); for any other domain the provider's callback is returned unchanged.
- **Errors:** 400 invalid, 502 unresolved.
- **Logs:** `lightning_address.resolved` (`cached`) and `lightning_address.resolve_failed`. The console line carries `addressRef` (or `address: "[address]"` while `SPEND_API_TOKEN` is unset), never the address; the diagnostic row keeps `address`.
- **Used by:** App donate `resolveLightningAddress`.
- **Auth:** See Purpose — Bearer where stated, else public.

## Endpoint: GET /me

- **Purpose:** Bearer session. Current owner account JSON (id, linkingKey, role, name, `username` (`string | null` LUD-16 / NIP-05 local-part), `location` (`string | null`, never omit, never `""`), `lightningAddress` (the receiving address: the verified in-app wallet `<username>@<host of PUBLIC_BASE_URL>`, or `null`; name kept for app compatibility), `lightningAddressVerified` (true iff `lightningAddress` is set), forumLawsDismissed, `createdAt`, `rulesAgreedAt`, owner `viewKey`, `setup`, `missing`, `hasPosted`, `aboutMe`, `aboutMeHasPhoto`, `aboutMessageId`, `notificationLevel`, `notifyHearts`, `amountUnit`, `locale`, `fiat`, `funding`, `walletRequired`, `walletBackupSeenAt`, `walletIntroSeen` (boolean, `false` until `POST /me/wallet-intro-seen`, owner-only), `hadExternalWallet` (boolean, true when the account has a stored legacy external payout address `account.lightning_address`; read-only, owner-only), `sparkPubkey` (`string | null` wallet identity key), `sparkWalletVerified` (true when `sparkPubkeyVerifiedAt` is a number), `passkeyCredentialId`, `passkeyRenewFailed`, `passkeyRenewClosed`, `passkeyRenewPrfUnsupported`, `socialRecoverySetUp`). `socialRecoverySetUp` is true when the api holds a 21.gifts recovery share for the account's current verified wallet (`sparkPubkey` with `sparkWalletVerified`); it has no grant or date check and is owner-only. `passkeyRenewFailed` is true only when that account has a failed renew row whose `acknowledged_at` is null. `passkeyRenewClosed` is true only when `walletRequired` is not true and a failed renew row has non-null `acknowledged_at`. `passkeyRenewPrfUnsupported` is true only when the newest unacknowledged failed renew row has error name `prfUnsupported`. A later seed (`walletRequired` true) is not closed. While it is true the account still has no seed. All three are owner-only. `sparkPubkey` / `sparkWalletVerified` are owner-only (omitted from public member cards and view profiles). `hasPosted` is true when the account has a live forum row that is not the profile note (replies still count) OR when `aboutMe` is non-null. A profile note that is only the display-name copy, a photo without bio text, a missing note, and a soft-hidden note do not count. Not the same predicate as GET /invoices/posted (that stays top-level non-profile only). `aboutMe` is the profile-note text when it is a real bio, else `null` (missing or soft-hidden (`deletedAt` set); auto name-copy is not a bio). `aboutMeHasPhoto` is true when the live profile note has a stored photo. `aboutMessageId` is the live profile-note id only when `aboutMe !== null`, else `null` (never `profileMessageId` under another name). `notificationLevel` is the owner fan-out filter (`all` \| `active` \| `mentions` \| `none`, default `all`, owner-only). `notifyHearts` is the owner heart-tip notification preference (boolean, default `true`, owner-only). `amountUnit` is the owner amount-entry unit (`btc` \| `fiat`, default `btc`, owner-only). `locale` is the owner UI language (`en` \| `de` \| `es` \| `fil`, nullable, null when unset, owner/debug only, omitted from public member cards and view profiles). `fiat` is the owner fiat display currency (`CHF` \| `EUR` \| `USD` \| `PHP`, nullable, null when unset, owner/debug only, omitted from public member cards and view profiles). `funding` is `null` for `basis`; otherwise always an object (`status: 'none'` when there is no row). `setup` is the next wizard step (`name` \| `username` \| `lightning-address` \| `rules`) or `null` when complete; `lightning-address` is the receiving-wallet step, done once the wallet is verified (or skipped); the api never returns `wallet`. Username is not skippable. The recovery phrase is not a setup step and does not change `setup` or `missing`. Skip timestamps count as done for name and the receiving wallet, not username. `missing` lists factually unset fields (`name`, `username`, `lightning-address` until the wallet is verified, `rules`) even when skipped, and never includes `wallet`. Does not expose `profileMessageId`. Location is not a setup step. An account with `sessionRefused` is 403 (not 401) so the client can sign the visitor out.
- **Errors:** 401 if missing/expired; 403 `{ error: 'You signed in with the wrong account. Please try again with the correct account.' }` when the bearer belongs to an account with `sessionRefused`.
- **Used by:** App `fetchMe`.
- **Auth:** See Purpose — Bearer where stated, else public.

## Endpoint: GET /members/:accountId

- **Purpose:** Bearer required. Live member profile card for `:accountId` (UUID): `id`, `name`, `username` (`string | null` LUD-16 / NIP-05 local-part), `location` (`string | null`, never omit, never `""`), `role`, `lightningAddress` (the wallet address `<username>@<host of PUBLIC_BASE_URL>` for a verified wallet with the LNURL server on, otherwise `null`; a stored external address is never returned), ISO `createdAt`, `profileMessage` (`serializeMessage` with `accountId` / `replyCount` like the signed-in forum list, or `null` when no note or when the profile note is soft-hidden via `deletedAt`), derived `aboutMe` (profile-note text when it is a real bio, else `null` when the profile note is missing or soft-hidden via `deletedAt` (same as `profileMessage`); auto name-copy is not a bio; keep `profileMessage`), `aboutMeHasPhoto` (true when the live profile note has a stored photo; false when `profileMessage` is null), uncapped live `postCount` / `replyCount` from `countByAccount` (not the latest-200 window), `trust` (`accountTrust`: `verifiedBy` / `proposedBy` / `confirmedBy` / `appointedBy`, each `{ id, name }` or `null`; all-null when no stored edges), and `fundingReviewedAt` (`grant.admittedAt` when the effective grant is admitted, else `null`; does not expose pending/trial/rejected) and `fundingReviewedByName` (live display name of `decidedBy` when that time is set and the trimmed name is non-empty, else `null`). Soft-hide does **not** clear `account.profileMessageId`. Member GET keeps `profileMessage` and does not include `aboutMessageId`. Never includes `viewKey`, linkingKey, npub, nsec, or `eventId`.
- **Shop count:** JSON always includes uncapped `shopCount` from `countShopsByAccount`, including 0; it is not derived from the capped shops list. A live top-level shop belongs to its assigned shop account when set and otherwise to its author.
- **Credit count:** JSON always includes uncapped `creditCount` from `countCreditsByAccount`, including 0; it is not derived from the capped credits list. A live top-level credit belongs to its author (`goalRepayable` exactly true); funded, unfunded, and still-live repaid notes all count; deleted notes and replies do not.
- **Errors:** 401 without session; 409 `{ error: 'missing_requirements', missing: [...] }` when `requireAction(caller, 'forum.read')` fails; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for a non-UUID id or unknown account; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` when a store throws (`members.get.failed`).
- **Used by:** App member profile surfaces.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session.

## Endpoint: GET /members/:accountId/posts

- **Purpose:** Bearer required. Live-only top-level notes by `:accountId` newest-first, capped at 200 (`listPostsByAccount`). Same `serializeMessage` as signed-in `GET /messages` (`accountId`, `replyCount`, `payable` when a non-empty `eventId` and a receiving address (`receivingAddress`: a verified wallet with the LNURL server configured) are set, `authorCanReceive` (true when the author has a receiving address, signed or not)). Omits `parentId`. Omits `goalSats` when unset (null/0/absent); includes the key only when a positive whole-sat goal is stored on a top-level note. Includes `goalRepayable: true` only when stored true (omitted when null, never false) and `goalTermDays` only when stored (omitted when null). Includes `goalCurrency`, `goalAmount`, and the four `goalAmount*` snapshots only when `goalCurrency` is stored (a snapshot may be null); a legacy row omits them. Replies by that member are not listed. A `hasVideo` row whose file is missing or empty is deleted and omitted. A credit note (`goalRepayable`) with sats is never deleted: `deleteById` returns false, nothing is logged, and the row is read again and stays listed while it is live. For each kept top-level note, missing-file `hasVideo` direct replies in the replies window (cap 200) are deleted (`messages.video.dropped`); `replyCount` is the live direct-reply count of attributed children (a 21.gifts author, or an external row whose `author_pubkey` is a recorded zapper in `nostr_zapper`) minus those dropped.
- **Errors:** 401 without session; 409 `{ error: 'missing_requirements', missing: [...] }` when `requireAction(caller, 'forum.read')` fails; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for a non-UUID id or unknown account; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` when a store throws (`members.posts.failed`).
- **Used by:** App member profile post feed.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session.

## Endpoint: GET /members/:accountId/shops

- **Purpose:** Lists the member's live top-level `#21GiftsShop` notes newest-first, capped at 200. Assignment to a shop account owns the note; an unassigned shop belongs to its author. JSON uses the same serialization as the posts feed, including `accountId`, attributed `replyCount`, conditional `payable`, `authorCanReceive` (true when the author has a receiving address, signed or not), `heartCount`, and `hearted`, while omitting `parentId`.
- **Errors:** 401 without a session; 409 when forum-read requirements are missing; 404 for a non-UUID or unknown member; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` on store failure, logged as `members.shops.failed`.
- **Used by:** App member profile shop feed.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session.

## Endpoint: GET /members/:accountId/credits

- **Purpose:** Lists the member's live top-level credit notes newest-first, capped at 200. The author owns the note (`goalRepayable` exactly true); shop assignment and hashtags do not move ownership. Funded, unfunded, and still-live repaid notes all count. JSON uses the same serialization as the posts feed, including `accountId`, attributed `replyCount`, conditional `payable`, `authorCanReceive` (true when the author has a receiving address, signed or not), `heartCount`, and `hearted`, while omitting `parentId`.
- **Errors:** 401 without a session; 409 when forum-read requirements are missing; 404 for a non-UUID or unknown member; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` on store failure, logged as `members.credits.failed`.
- **Used by:** App member profile credit feed.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session.

## Endpoint: GET /members/:accountId/replies

- **Purpose:** Bearer required. Live-only replies by `:accountId` newest-first, capped at 200 (`listRepliesByAccount`). `serializeMessage` with `payable` when a non-empty `eventId` and a receiving address (`receivingAddress`: a verified wallet with the LNURL server configured) are set (same as posts), `authorCanReceive` (true when the author has a receiving address, signed or not), `accountId`, and optional `parentId` when set; omits `replyCount`. Omits `goalSats`, `goalRepayable`, and `goalTermDays` (replies never include them). Top-level notes by that member are not listed. A `hasVideo` row whose file is missing or empty is deleted and omitted. A child that cannot serialize (invalid `createdAt`) is omitted; remaining siblings still 200 `{ messages }`.
- **Errors:** 401 without session; 409 `{ error: 'missing_requirements', missing: [...] }` when `requireAction(caller, 'forum.read')` fails; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for a non-UUID id or unknown account; 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` when a store throws (`members.replies.failed`). Invalid `createdAt` on one child is not 503.
- **Used by:** App member profile reply feed.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session.

## Endpoint: GET /view/:viewKey

- **Purpose:** Public capability URL. The same visible profile as `GET /members/:accountId`: `id`, `name`, `username` (`string | null`), `location` (`string | null`, never omit, never `""`), `role`, `lightningAddress` and `lightningAddressVerified` (the wallet address and `true` for a verified wallet with the LNURL server on, otherwise `null` and `false`; a stored external address is ignored), epoch-ms `createdAt`, `hasPasskey`, `aboutMe`, `aboutMeHasPhoto`, `aboutMessageId`, `profileMessage` (`serializeMessage` plus `heartCount` and `hearted`, or `null`; `hearted` is false because there is no viewer), uncapped `postCount`, `replyCount`, `shopCount`, `creditCount`, `fundingReviewedAt` (`null` unless admitted), `fundingReviewedByName` (`null` when unnamed or not admitted), and `staffTag` only when it is `software_developer`. Omits `linkingKey`, `viewKey`, the moderator `trust` graph, and owner settings. `hasPasskey` is true when the account already has a passkey credential. `aboutMe` is the profile-note text when it is a real bio, else `null`. `aboutMessageId` is the live profile-note id only when `aboutMe !== null`, else `null`. No auth. Not a session. Lists are `GET /view/:viewKey/posts`, `/shops`, `/credits`, and `/replies`.
- **Errors:** 404 `{ "error": "Not found" }` when the param is not 64 lowercase hex or the key is unknown. 503 `{ "error": "Messages are unavailable" }` when the profile-note read, the post, reply, shop, or credit count, the funding lookup, or the reviewer account lookup throws (`view.get.failed`).
- **Used by:** Anyone with the link (owner copies `viewKey` from GET `/me`); invite page uses `hasPasskey` for the activation banner.
- **Auth:** none.

## Endpoint: GET /view/:viewKey/posts

- **Purpose:** Public. Same `{ messages }` as `GET /members/:accountId/posts` for the account behind the view key. `hearted` is false. No auth.
- **Errors:** 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for a bad or unknown key. 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` when the store throws (`view.posts.failed`).
- **Used by:** The public profile page.
- **Auth:** none.

## Endpoint: GET /view/:viewKey/shops

- **Purpose:** Public. Same `{ messages }` as `GET /members/:accountId/shops` for the account behind the view key. `hearted` is false. No auth.
- **Errors:** 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for a bad or unknown key. 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` when the store throws (`view.shops.failed`).
- **Used by:** The public profile page.
- **Auth:** none.

## Endpoint: GET /view/:viewKey/credits

- **Purpose:** Public. Same `{ messages }` as `GET /members/:accountId/credits` for the account behind the view key. `hearted` is false. No auth.
- **Errors:** 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for a bad or unknown key. 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` when the store throws (`view.credits.failed`).
- **Used by:** The public profile page.
- **Auth:** none.

## Endpoint: GET /view/:viewKey/replies

- **Purpose:** Public. Same `{ messages }` as `GET /members/:accountId/replies` for the account behind the view key. `hearted` is false. A reply that cannot serialize is omitted. No auth.
- **Errors:** 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` for a bad or unknown key. 503 `{ error: 'Messages are unavailable' }` when the store throws (`view.replies.failed`).
- **Used by:** The public profile page.
- **Auth:** none.

## Endpoint: GET /messages

- **Purpose:** Signed-in list after `requireAction(account, 'forum.read')` (needs rules). With no `Authorization` header, `mode=active`, no hashtag, and no country, the same path is the public window (see Auth) and is 200, not 401. Lists **top-level** forum notes only (`parent_id` null, `deleted_at` null) via `listFeed`. A profile note is omitted only when its trimmed text equals the display name or the name stored on the note (case-insensitive) and it has no photo, no extra stills, and no video.
