## Endpoint: GET /debug/trust-edges

- **Purpose:** Operator listing of every stored trust edge (`serializeTrustEdge`), newest `createdAt` then `id` descending. Success JSON is `{ edges }` (`serializeTrustEdge` rows).
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 503 `{ error: 'Trust chain is unavailable' }` on unexpected store throw (`debug.trust_edges.failed`).
- **Used by:** Operator `gifts-debug trust-edges`.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: POST /debug/trust-edges

- **Purpose:** Operator backfill of a stored trust edge. Body `{ "subjectId", "actorId", "kind" }` with `kind` one of `verify` / `moderator_propose` / `moderator_confirm` / `moderator_appoint` / `moderator_reject`. Inserts the edge, logs `debug.trust_edges.inserted` `{ subjectId, actorId, kind }`, and returns `{ id, subjectId, actorId, kind, createdAt }` (`createdAt` ISO-8601). Does **not** change `account.role`. `PATCH /debug/accounts/:id` remains role-only. POST 409 duplicate only for live-unique kinds (`verify` / `moderator_confirm` / `moderator_appoint`); propose and reject may repeat.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 400 `{ error: 'Expected a JSON body with "subjectId", "actorId", and "kind" strings' }`; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` when subject or actor is missing or not a UUID; 409 `{ error: 'Conflict' }` on duplicate live-unique `(subjectId, kind)` or `subjectId === actorId`; 503 `{ error: 'Trust chain is unavailable' }` on unexpected store throw (`debug.trust_edges.failed`).
- **Used by:** Operator `gifts-debug trust-edge` CLI.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: DELETE /debug/trust-edges

- **Purpose:** Operator delete of a stored trust edge. Body `{ "subjectId", "kind" }` with `kind` one of `verify` / `moderator_propose` / `moderator_confirm` / `moderator_appoint` / `moderator_reject`. Removes the latest `(subjectId, kind)` row (`createdAt` desc, then `id` desc), logs `debug.trust_edges.deleted` `{ subjectId, kind }`, and returns the deleted `{ id, subjectId, actorId, kind, createdAt }` (`createdAt` ISO-8601). Does **not** change `account.role`.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 400 `{ error: 'Expected a JSON body with "subjectId" and "kind" strings' }`; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` when `subjectId` is not a UUID or no row matches; 503 `{ error: 'Trust chain is unavailable' }` on unexpected store throw (`debug.trust_edges.delete_failed`).
- **Used by:** Operator `gifts-debug trust-edge-delete` CLI.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: GET /debug/dump

- **Purpose:** Operator catalog of every allowlisted Postgres table as camelCase JSON (cap 200 per table). Success JSON is `{ tables }` keyed by allowlisted table name: `account`, `passkey_credential`, `passkey_challenge`, `auth_session`, `api_log`, `contact`, `pos_charge`, `conversation`, `conversation_message`, `conversation_read`, `message`, `message_extra_photo`, `shop_identity`, `message_invoice`, `nostr_zap_ingest`, `nostr_zap_receipt`, `nostr_zap_payment`, `nostr_zapper`, `nostr_blocked_pubkey`, `notification`, `push_subscription`, `push_outbox`, `trust_edge`, `gift`, `btc_usd_daily`, `usd_fiat_daily`, `db_change`. Media bytes stay off JSON (`photoBytes` / extra-photo `bytes` are lengths). Only `account` rows carry `nostrNsecCiphertext` (envelope hex, never decrypted); a `shop_identity` row has the key metadata and the outbox state but never its encrypted secret. `btc_usd_daily` / `usd_fiat_daily` dump stored rate rows when the rate books expose `listDebug`; `db_change` dumps when a list port is wired (in-memory boots dump `[]`). `api_log` dumps when `apiLogStore` is wired (the newest 200; the full history is `GET /debug/api-log` following `before` and `beforeId` while `hasMore` is true).
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 503 `{ error: 'Dump is unavailable' }` when a store throws.
- **Used by:** Operator `gifts-debug dump`.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: GET /debug/dump/:table

- **Purpose:** Same catalog as `GET /debug/dump` for one allowlisted table name. Response `{ table, rows }`.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Debug is not configured' }` when `DEBUG_TOKEN` is unset or blank; 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` when the Bearer token does not match; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` when the table name is not allowlisted (`account`, `passkey_credential`, `passkey_challenge`, `auth_session`, `api_log`, `contact`, `pos_charge`, `conversation`, `conversation_message`, `conversation_read`, `message`, `message_extra_photo`, `shop_identity`, `message_invoice`, `nostr_zap_ingest`, `nostr_zap_receipt`, `nostr_zap_payment`, `nostr_zapper`, `nostr_blocked_pubkey`, `notification`, `push_subscription`, `push_outbox`, `trust_edge`, `gift`, `btc_usd_daily`, `usd_fiat_daily`, `db_change`); 503 `{ error: 'Dump is unavailable' }` when a store throws.
- **Used by:** Operator `gifts-debug dump <table>`.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` with `DEBUG_TOKEN`. Not an end-user session.

## Endpoint: PUT /me/wallet

- **Purpose:** Bearer required. Body `{ sparkPubkey }` (66 hex characters, compressed secp256k1). Claims the wallet identity key on the account while unverified (`claimSparkPubkey`). A second PUT overwrites freely until verification. Mounted only when `LNURL_SERVER_URL` and `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` resolve; otherwise Hono's default 404. Logs `account.wallet.claimed` `{ accountId }` (never the key). Success is owner JSON including `sparkPubkey` and `sparkWalletVerified`.
- **Errors:** 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }`; 400 `{ error: 'Expected a JSON body with a "sparkPubkey" of 66 hex characters' }`; 409 `{ error: 'missing_requirements', missing: ['username'] }` without a username; 409 `{ error: 'Wallet is not set up' }` when `walletRequired` is not true; 409 `{ error: 'Wallet is already connected' }` once verified (or a concurrent verification). Not mounted → 404.
- **Used by:** App wallet bind after seed setup.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session.

## Endpoint: PUT /me/recovery-share

- **Purpose:** Stores the 21.gifts share of the member's social recovery set (docs/social-recovery.md). Body `{ "sparkPubkey": "<current verified wallet key>", "share": "<20-word SLIP-39 share>" }` (strict; the share is 20 lowercase words of 4–8 letters with single spaces, the raw mnemonic of member index 2, not the QR payload). The share is encrypted with `RECOVERY_SHARE_KEK` (AES-256-GCM, the account id as additional data, `encryptRecoveryShare`) and stored for that wallet key in one locked step (`putRecoveryShare`); a second PUT for the same wallet replaces it. Nothing in the api decrypts it. Success is 200 owner JSON with `socialRecoverySetUp: true`. Logs `account.recovery_share.stored` `{ accountId, replaced }`; never the share, the key, or the body. The first share of the current wallet (not a replacement) reads the grant and, when the member is eligible today with that share, sends the daily ping of today's newest media post (`pingTodayMedia` without a second share read); a grant read or ping failure logs `funding.daily_ping.failed` and the answer stays 200. Mounted only when `LNURL_SERVER_URL` and `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` resolve; otherwise Hono's default 404.
- **Errors:** 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }`; 503 `{ error: 'Social recovery is unavailable' }` without `RECOVERY_SHARE_KEK` (memory boot, checked before the body); 400 `{ error: 'Expected a JSON body with sparkPubkey and a recovery share' }` for bad JSON, a wrong shape, a key that does not normalize, or a share that is not 20 lowercase words; 409 `{ error: 'Wallet is not connected' }` while the wallet is not verified; 409 `{ error: 'Wallet changed' }` when `sparkPubkey` is not the account's verified wallet key, also when it changes before the locked write; 503 `{ error: 'Social recovery is unavailable' }` when encryption or the store fails (logs `account.recovery_share.failed` `{ accountId }` with the error name and code). Not mounted → 404.
- **Used by:** App `/settings/social-recovery` (secure my account), after the two holder shares are handed over.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session.

## Endpoint: GET /me/previous-wallets

- **Purpose:** The wallets the account had before a social recovery commit, newest first (`listPreviousWallets`): 200 `{ wallets: [{ sparkPubkey, sparkAddress, replacedAt, balanceSats }] }` (`replacedAt` epoch ms; empty array when none). `balanceSats` is the soft balance (`btcSoftBalanceSats`, unclaimed incoming transfers included) read anonymously from the public Sparkscan explorer for each wallet in parallel (`fetchSparkAddressBalance`, 5 s timeout, no API key), or `null` when unknown. It is information only: no action depends on it. Mounted only when `LNURL_SERVER_URL` and `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` resolve; otherwise Hono's default 404.
- **Errors:** 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }`; 503 `{ error: 'Wallet data is unavailable' }` when the store fails (logs `account.previous_wallets.failed` `{ accountId }`; the explorer is not called). An explorer failure is never an error: that wallet's `balanceSats` is `null` and `spark_explorer.unavailable` is logged without the address. Not mounted → 404.
- **Used by:** App settings (previous wallets row, `#previous-wallets`) and `/settings/previous-wallets/[sparkPubkey]`.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session.

## Endpoint: DELETE /me/previous-wallets/:sparkPubkey

- **Purpose:** Drops one previous wallet of the account together with the account's 21.gifts recovery share of that key, in one statement (`dropPreviousWallet`). The app offers it only after the wallet, opened with its words, read a balance of 0. Answers 204 with no body and logs `account.previous_wallet.dropped` `{ accountId }` (never the key). Mounted only when `LNURL_SERVER_URL` and `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` resolve; otherwise Hono's default 404.
- **Errors:** 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }`; 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` when `:sparkPubkey` does not normalize or is not a previous wallet of the account (also on a repeat); 503 `{ error: 'Wallet data is unavailable' }` when the store fails (logs `account.previous_wallets.failed` `{ accountId }`). Not mounted → 404.
- **Used by:** App `/settings/previous-wallets/[sparkPubkey]` (transfer remaining balance) once the wallet reads 0.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session.

## Endpoint: POST /lnurlpay/:pubkey

- **Purpose:** Forward wallet registration to the self-hosted LNURL server. Mounted only when `LNURL_SERVER_URL` and `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` resolve. Gate: normalised pubkey, JSON body with string `username`; `body.username` must consist only of printable ASCII characters (otherwise 404 and the LNURL server is not contacted); no other key equals `username` after NFKC normalisation and lower-casing (for example `Username`); normalised name; account owns that username with matching claimed key; and no other account is verified on the key. The body is forwarded as the parsed JSON re-serialised. Upstream 2xx marks the key verified (`account.wallet.verified`); when the account is not verified on that key afterwards, 409 `{ error: 'Wallet registration could not be confirmed' }` and `account.wallet.unconfirmed`. Path segments are only `A-Z a-z 0-9 . _ ~ -`, never `.` or `..`. Rate limit 30/min/client. Body cap 1 MB (1 048 576 bytes); a larger body gets 413 `{ error: 'Request body is too large' }` and the upstream is not contacted. A request without a validated `cf-connecting-ip` is not counted by the per-address limits.
- **Errors:** 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` when the gate fails, a path segment is refused, or the route is not mounted; 409 `{ error: 'Wallet registration could not be confirmed' }` when upstream accepted registration but the account is not verified on that key afterwards; 429 `{ error: 'Too many requests' }`; 413 `{ error: 'Request body is too large' }`; 503 `{ error: 'Lightning address service is unavailable' }` when upstream is unreachable, the store throws, or upstream returns a status outside 2xx and 4xx (for example 5xx). Upstream 2xx passed through with its status, body and only the `content-type` / `cache-control` headers (a 204 stays a 204). Upstream 4xx is passed through with body and status (nothing verified).
- **Used by:** In-app wallet registration of `username@<domain>`.
- **Auth:** none (wallet signature in body or `x-breez-*` headers; no api session).

## Endpoint: POST /lnurlpay/:pubkey/transfer

- **Purpose:** Forward the Lightning address transfer of a social recovery to the self-hosted LNURL server and commit the recovery when it moves the name. Mounted only when `LNURL_SERVER_URL` and `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` resolve. The path key is the new wallet key; the Breez SDK sends `{ username, description, from_pubkey, from_signature, to_signature, timestamp }`. Gate: one JSON object with no other key that NFKC-lowercases to one of those fields, a printable-ASCII username that normalizes, a description of at most 255 code points, a valid `from_pubkey`, string signatures, a timestamp within 600 s, both DER signatures valid for the `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` host (old key over `transfer-from`, new key over `transfer-to`), and the account's unforwarded, unexpired pending recovery with that new key, authorized by its old key, still the verified wallet key, for the account's username. Admission marks the recovery forwarded in the same step, with the time read after the body and the checks, so `forwardedAt` precedes the upstream send only by the admission step, not by the body upload or the checks (`account.recovery.forwarded`). The body is forwarded re-serialized. Upstream 2xx commits the recovery (new passkey only, sessions and push subscriptions deleted, old key to the previous wallets, new key verified with the first verification time; `account.recovery.committed` with `via: 'claim'`). Rate limit 30/min/client. Body cap 1 MB.
- **Errors:** 404 `{ error: 'Not found' }` when the gate fails, a path segment is refused, or the route is not mounted; 409 `{ error: 'Wallet registration could not be confirmed' }` when upstream accepted but the commit did not land, because the forwarded pending recovery is already gone or the account no longer holds the old key verified, in which case the commit deletes the pending recovery (`account.recovery.unconfirmed`; this request changes no account, and no pending recovery remains to settle); 429 `{ error: 'Too many requests' }`; 413 `{ error: 'Request body is too large' }`; 503 `{ error: 'Lightning address service is unavailable' }` when upstream is unreachable or times out, returns 5xx, or the store throws. Upstream 2xx and 4xx (for example 409 `"signature has already been used"`) pass through with body and status. After an upstream 4xx, 5xx or timeout, and after a store throw in the commit, the pending recovery stays forwarded and the account unchanged. A store throw at admission (`markRecoveryForwarded`) leaves the pending recovery unforwarded and contacts no upstream.
- **Used by:** In-app wallet (Breez SDK `claimLightningAddressTransfer`) during social recovery.
- **Auth:** none (both wallets' signatures in the body; no api session).

## Endpoint: POST /lnurlpay/:pubkey/recover

- **Purpose:** Forward signed recover to the self-hosted LNURL server. Mounted only when `LNURL_SERVER_URL` and `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` resolve. Gate: normalised pubkey and `isSparkPubkeyClaimed`, or, for a key no account claims, the new key of an unexpired pending social recovery. Rate limit 30/min/client. Body cap 1 MB (1 048 576 bytes); a larger body gets 413 `{ error: 'Request body is too large' }` and the upstream is not contacted. After the upstream answer, a forwarded pending recovery is settled (`settleAfterLookup`): when the lookup was sent more than 30 s after the forward, the new key holding the username or a verified "user not found" for the old key commits it, and the old key still holding the username drops it. The answer to the wallet is unchanged.
- **Errors:** 404 when the gate fails or the route is not mounted; 429; 413; 503 when unreachable, the store throws, or upstream returns any status other than 2xx and 404 (other 4xx and 5xx). Upstream 2xx passed through with its status, body and only the `content-type` / `cache-control` headers (a 204 stays a 204); upstream 404 → 404.
- **Used by:** In-app wallet recover of a previously registered name.
- **Auth:** none (wallet signature headers/body; no api session).

## Endpoint: GET /lnurlpay/:pubkey/metadata

- **Purpose:** Forward signed metadata for received payments. Mounted only when `LNURL_SERVER_URL` and `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` resolve. Gate: normalised pubkey and a verified account on that key. Raw query string forwarded unchanged. Rate limit 120/min/client. A `HEAD` request is answered 404 and the LNURL server is not contacted.
- **Errors:** 404 when the gate fails or the route is not mounted; 429; 503 when unreachable, the store throws, or upstream returns any status other than 2xx and 404 (other 4xx and 5xx). Upstream 2xx passed through with its status, body and only the `content-type` / `cache-control` headers (a 204 stays a 204); upstream 404 → 404.
- **Used by:** In-app wallet payment history.
- **Auth:** none (wallet signature headers; no api session).

## Endpoint: GET /lnurlp/:username/invoice

- **Purpose:** Forward LNURL-pay invoice fetch for a wallet-backed username. Mounted only when `LNURL_SERVER_URL` and `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` resolve. Gate: normalised username and account with verified wallet. Raw query string forwarded unchanged. Rate limit 20/min/client. CORS `*`. A `HEAD` request is answered 404 and the LNURL server is not contacted. The account's pending point-of-sale charge is read before the upstream call; after an upstream 2xx whose `pr` is a BOLT11 for exactly that charge's millisat amount, its payment hash is recorded against that charge for the paid watcher (`recordInvoice`, one payment hash claim transaction). When another charge's invoice row or another shop payment already holds that hash (`other_payment`), the BOLT11 is not handed out: the route logs `shop.alert.duplicate_invoice` (`chargeId`, `holderChargeId`, and `paymentId` only when a shop payment holds it) and answers 503. Every other outcome leaves the response unchanged, and a store failure only logs `pos.invoice.record_failed` and still hands the invoice out.
- **Errors:** 404 when the gate fails or the route is not mounted; 429; 503 when unreachable, the store throws, upstream returns any status other than 2xx and 404 (other 4xx and 5xx), or another payment holds the payment hash of the BOLT11 recorded for the pending charge. Upstream 2xx passed through with its status, body and only the `content-type` / `cache-control` headers (a 204 stays a 204), including LUD-06 `{ status: 'ERROR', reason }` as HTTP 200 from upstream.
- **Used by:** Payers fetching an invoice for `username@<domain>` after the well-known payRequest.
- **Auth:** none.

## Endpoint: GET /lnurlp/cb/:username

- **Purpose:** Shop payment callback (`ShopPayments.callback`). Mounted only when `LNURL_SERVER_URL` and `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` resolve, and registered after `GET /lnurlp/:username/invoice`, so `/lnurlp/cb/invoice` stays the invoice forward of user `cb`. Gate: normalised username that is not `invoice`, and an account with a verified wallet. Without `method` it is the LUD-06 second step for the open amount: `amount` equal to the payment's millisats returns `{ pr, routes: [] }` with the payment's BOLT11; it never mints. With `method`, the quote must be the current or previous quote of the open amount and not expired; `Lightning`/`BTC` returns `{ pr }`, and an offered stablecoin method and asset (case-insensitive, `available` in the quote's `transferAmounts`) returns `offerDetail`: `{ expiryDate, blockchain, uri, hint }` with an EIP-681 `ethereum:<token>@<chainId>/transfer?address=<deposit>&uint256=<amountIn>` URI and a hint that ends with `<PUBLIC_BASE_URL>/lnurlp/tx/<username>`. The first detail of an offer sets `detail_issued_at` and, when unset, the payment's `next_history_at` 10 s ahead. Rate limit 120/min/client. CORS `*`, methods `GET, OPTIONS`, `Cache-Control: no-store`. A `HEAD` request is answered 404.
- **Errors:** All bodies are `shopErrorBody`, except that an unmounted route answers the default plain-text `404 Not Found`. 503 `Too many requests` over the limit (not 429); 404 `Not found` when the gate fails; 400 `Amount does not match this payment` on the amount path without an open amount or with another amount; 400 `That payment is not offered` when `quote` or `asset` is missing, a `quote`, `method`, `asset`, or `amount` key is repeated, or the pair is not offered on that quote; 404 `Quote expired` when there is no open amount or the quote is not a UUID, unknown, older than the previous one, or expired; 503 `Lightning address service is unavailable` when the store throws (logs `lnurl_server.failed`).
- **Used by:** OpenCryptoPay wallets (Tether Wallet reads 400 as `RAIL_UNAVAILABLE` and 404 as `QUOTE_EXPIRED`), LNURL wallets paying the open amount, the payment page for the Lightning detail, and the pay link's simplified flow.
- **Auth:** none.

## Endpoint: GET /lnurlp/tx/:username

- **Purpose:** Shop payment proof (`handleShopProof` in `src/lib/shop-proof.ts`). Mounted only when `LNURL_SERVER_URL` and `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` resolve. Gate as the callback. Query `quote`, `method`, optional `asset`, and exactly one of `tx` (64 hex, optional `0x`) or `hex` (signed EVM transaction, 100 to 4096 hex characters, optional `0x`). A stored proof of the quote with that hash is a repeat and answers from its row after filling in a missing log, sending a `hex` that was never sent, and resubmitting an unresolved row (at most every 2 s, 5 per minute per proof). Otherwise `tx` is verified with `eth_getTransactionReceipt`, else `eth_getUserOperationReceipt`, and the ERC-20 `Transfer` log to an offer's deposit address selects the offer; `hex` is decoded and checked against an offer's transfer data and `minFee`, stored, broadcast with `eth_sendRawTransaction`, and its receipt read once. Every verified proof is stored and, once it has a log index, submitted to Orchestra (`{ quoteId, txHash }`): a `hex` proof only after its receipt with status `0x1` was found, so a new `hex` proof without a receipt yet answers 503 `Payment is being confirmed` and the watcher submits it after the receipts duty fills its log index. The whole request stays within 18 s (3 s per RPC call, 10 s for Orchestra). 200 `{ txId }` with the transaction hash once the proof is `accepted`. Rate limit 120/min/client; over it a stored proof is still answered from its row. CORS `*`, methods `GET, OPTIONS`, `Cache-Control: no-store`. A `HEAD` request is answered 404.
- **Errors:** All bodies are `shopErrorBody`, except that an unmounted route answers the default plain-text `404 Not Found`. 404 `Quote expired` when the gate fails (an invalid or unknown username, or no verified wallet: no quote of that username exists; 503 `Too many requests` when also over the limit); over the limit every other refusal and a proof that is not stored are 503 `Too many requests`; 400 `Payment was not accepted` for a repeated key, a missing or unknown `method`, neither or both of `tx` and `hex`, a malformed value, a failed receipt, no matching transfer log, a `hex` that fails its checks or is rejected on broadcast, or a refused proof; 400 `Transaction already used` when every matching log belongs to another quote's proof or the proof is refused as `tx_used` (when another offer's row already holds that deposit: the row being filled in while a proof is stored, or the newer of the two rows when a submit or status read returned the other row's order; also when it was `accepted`); 404 `Quote expired` for a quote that is not a UUID or not one of this username's; 503 `Payment is being confirmed` for a new proof without the stablecoin configuration or an RPC endpoint for the chain (nothing is stored; a stored proof answers from its row), and when the gas price for an EIP-1559 `hex` cannot be read; 503 `Transfer not found yet` when no receipt exists yet (a lookup row is stored, at most 4 rows per quote, done ones included); 503 `Payment is being confirmed` while the proof is unresolved, held, or `stuck`; 400 `Another transfer is paying this payment` (`superseded`), `Invoice expired` (`late`), `The transfer was refunded` (`refunded`), `The transfer was not completed` (`failed`); 503 when the budget runs out (the watcher continues); 503 `Payment is being confirmed` when the store throws (logs `lnurl_server.failed`).
- **Used by:** OpenCryptoPay wallets after paying the stablecoin detail (Tether Wallet retries only on 5xx and reads any status below 500 as final).
- **Auth:** none.

## Endpoint: GET /lnurlp/wait/:payment

- **Purpose:** Shop payment status for the payment page (`ShopPayments.wait`). Mounted only when `LNURL_SERVER_URL` and `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` resolve. `:payment` must be a UUID (`normalizeUuid`, looked up in lowercase). 200 `waitBody`: `{ status, paymentStatus, invoiceExpiresAt }`, where `paymentStatus` is `open`, `accepted`, `paid`, or `ended`, `status` is DFX's `Pending`, `Completed` (paid), `Cancelled` (closed with `superseded`, `replaced`, or `cancelled`), or `Expired` (other closed), and `invoiceExpiresAt` is the BOLT11 expiry. With `since=<paymentStatus>` it holds while the stored status equals `since` and `status` is `Pending`, re-reading every `WAIT_SATS_POLL_MS` (250 ms) up to `WAIT_SATS_TIMEOUT_MS` (25 s). Rate limit 120/min/client. CORS `*`, `Cache-Control: no-store`. A `HEAD` request is answered 404.
- **Errors:** All bodies are `shopErrorBody`, except that an unmounted route answers the default plain-text `404 Not Found`. 429 `Too many requests` over the limit; 404 `Not found` for a malformed or unknown id; 503 `Lightning address service is unavailable` when the store throws (logs `lnurl_server.failed`).
- **Used by:** The app's payment page, polling after it opened or showed a shop payment.
- **Auth:** none.

## Endpoint: GET /verify/:paymentHash

- **Purpose:** LUD-21 payment verification forward to the self-hosted LNURL server. Mounted only when `LNURL_SERVER_URL` and `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` resolve. Forwarded when the payment hash segment is only `A-Z a-z 0-9 . _ ~ -` and is neither `.` nor `..` (joined as-is, no percent-encoding). No query string. Rate limit 120/min/client. CORS `*`. A `HEAD` request is answered 404 and the LNURL server is not contacted.
- **Errors:** 404 when the route is not mounted or the payment hash segment is refused (upstream not contacted); 429; 503 when unreachable (network / timeout / redirect / body-read failure) or upstream returns any status other than 2xx and 404 (other 4xx and 5xx). Upstream 2xx passed through with its status, body and only the `content-type` / `cache-control` headers (a 204 stays a 204); upstream 404 → 404.
- **Used by:** Wallets verifying a paid invoice.
- **Auth:** none.

## Endpoint: POST /me/wallet-backup-seen

- **Purpose:** Bearer required. Empty body. Sets `walletBackupSeenAt` via `markWalletBackupSeen` (`WHERE wallet_backup_seen_at IS NULL`; logs `account.wallet.backup_seen` `{ accountId }` only when this call wrote). Does not change `walletRequired`. Owner JSON including `setup` / `missing` / `funding`. Never a mnemonic or PRF.
- **Errors:** 401 without session.
- **Used by:** App `POST /me/wallet-backup-seen` records that this account can show a recovery phrase. Not a confirmation and not a setup step. Empty body. Does not change `walletRequired`.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session.

## Endpoint: POST /me/passkey-renew/report

- **Purpose:** Bearer required. Strict JSON `{ stage, outcome, errorName, errorCode, httpStatus, message }` plus optional public authenticator facts (`authenticatorAttachment`, `transports`, `aaguid`, `prfEnabled`, `prfPresent`, `extensions`, `authenticatorFlags`, `publicKeyAlgorithm`, `residentKey`, `hmacSecret`, `credProtect`, `clientCapabilities`). Accepts only `failed` or `cancelled` (`succeeded` is 400). Stores a passkey renew row (server id and `createdAt`; User-Agent from the header). Does not change the account row. Before the length cap, a phrase of 12 or more whitespace-separated tokens, or a run of 64 or more token characters, is stored as `[redacted]` on error name (80), error code (80), message (500), and user agent (300). Debug values outside the allowlist are stored as null and do not reject the row. `authenticatorFlags` is the WebAuthn flags byte (UP 0x01, UV 0x04, BE 0x08, BS 0x10, AT 0x40, ED 0x80). Never stores a credential id, attestation, challenge, or PRF output. Returns owner JSON loaded after the insert, including `passkeyRenewFailed`, `passkeyRenewClosed`, and `passkeyRenewPrfUnsupported` (true only for the newest unacknowledged `prfUnsupported` row). A seed that landed during the request is not closed. `passkeyRenewClosed` is true only while `walletRequired` is false.
- **Errors:** 401 without session; 400 for a bad report body (invalid JSON, unknown keys, or `outcome: "succeeded"`).
- **Used by:** App passkey renew ceremony (browser cancel / PRF failure) via `POST /me/passkey-renew/report`.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session.

## Endpoint: POST /me/passkey-renew/ack

- **Purpose:** Bearer required. Empty body is fine. Acknowledges failed unacknowledged renew rows only (`outcome = 'failed'` and `acknowledged_at` IS NULL). Does not change the account row. Returns owner JSON loaded after the acknowledgement, including `passkeyRenewFailed`, `passkeyRenewClosed`, and `passkeyRenewPrfUnsupported` (true only when the newest unacknowledged failed renew row has error name `prfUnsupported`; acknowledgement clears that row, so the flag is false). A seed that landed during the request is not closed. `passkeyRenewClosed` is true only while `walletRequired` is false.
- **Errors:** 401 without session.
- **Used by:** App after the owner dismisses a failed renew.
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session.

## Endpoint: POST /me/setup/skip

- **Purpose:** Bearer required. Body `{ step: "name" | "lightning-address" }`. Sets `nameSkippedAt` or `lightningAddressSkippedAt` to now so owner `setup` advances; does not clear or change `name` or the wallet. `lightning-address` is the receiving-wallet step (token kept for app compatibility). Skipping an already-set field is allowed (writes the skip timestamp). Rules cannot be skipped. `step: "wallet"` is 400 like an unknown step.
- **Errors:** 401 without session; 400 for unknown step, `step: "wallet"`, `step: "rules"`, or bad JSON (same copy as an invalid step).
- **Used by:** App onboarding skip controls (api-first; app proxy may follow later).
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer` session.

## Endpoint: GET /debug/external-pubkeys

Operator inspection of external Nostr identities that have earned visibility or have been blocked.

- **Auth:** Requires `Authorization: Bearer <DEBUG_TOKEN>`; unset or blank configuration returns 503 and a mismatch returns 401.
- **Response:** Returns `{ zappers, blocked }`, with lowercase pubkeys, receipt/message references, staff id, and ISO timestamps; both arrays are newest first.
- **Limits:** Each array is independently capped at 200 rows, matching the other operator debug listings.
- **Failure:** A store read failure returns 503 `{ "error": "External pubkeys are unavailable" }` and no partial list.

## Endpoint: GET /links/:code

- **Purpose:** Public resolver: an 8-hex prefix of a forum-message UUID or account UUID becomes exactly one full id. Website short links are `https://<origin>/l/<8 hex>` and call this to learn where that code points (`kind` is `message` or `member`).
- **Errors:** 400 `{ error: 'invalid_code' }` when `:code` is not exactly eight hex digits after `toLowerCase()` (no trim); 404 `{ error: 'not_found' }` when neither store has a match; 409 `{ error: 'ambiguous' }` when two or more ids match (two messages, two accounts, or one of each). No ids in error bodies. No 503 path.
- **Used by:** Website short-link landing (`/l/<8 hex>`).
- **Auth:** none. Public. Soft-hidden messages are included; the public message page decides who may see them.

## Endpoint: GET /habits

- **Purpose:** Public list of member habits. No bearer required. A valid bearer includes `notes` only on the caller's own habits. `notes` is omitted for everyone else. An invalid or unknown bearer is treated as signed out and still returns the public list. Periods run from `firstPeriod` through the latest ratable day or week and stop at `lastPeriod`. `logged` is false and `status` is null when the owner has not recorded that period.
- **Inputs:** Optional `Authorization: Bearer`. No query and no body.
- **Returns / side effects:** 200 `{ reviewWeek: { start }, habits }`. `reviewWeek.start` is the Manila review Monday. Each comment carries `canReceive`: true when its author has a receiving address (`receivingAddress`), false otherwise; all distinct comment authors are read in one bulk account lookup (`getAccounts`) per request. No writes.
- **Errors:** 503 `{ error: 'Habits are unavailable' }` when the store throws (`habits.failed`). A bad bearer is not an error.
- **Used by:** the habit tracker page.

## Endpoint: POST /habits

- **Purpose:** One strict JSON action: `add`, `edit`, `archive`, `log`, `comment`, `deleteComment`, `invoice`. Bearer required. `add` requires `Time-Zone`. `comment`, `deleteComment`, and `invoice` follow the same Sunday rest as other public writing and as `POST /messages/:id/invoice`: 403 `{ error: 'SUNDAY_REST' }` when `Time-Zone` names an IANA zone that is Sunday. The invoice check is before the amount check. A missing, blank, or invalid zone does not refuse. Rating, add, edit, and archive are not that refusal. An edit whose current period is after `lastPeriod` is 409 `{ error: 'Period is closed' }` and does not change the stored wording. An edit of another member's habit is 404, including when that period is already closed. `invoice` returns `{ pr, amountSats }` minted on the comment author's receiving address (the verified in-app wallet) and does not pay. Comments are not forum posts.
- **Inputs:** Bearer session. Body is one action object. `add`, `comment`, `deleteComment`, and `invoice` also send `Time-Zone`.
- **Returns / side effects:** 201 `{ ok: true, id }` for add. 201 `{ ok: true }` for comment, with no id. 200 `{ ok: true }` for edit, archive, log, and deleteComment. 200 `{ pr, amountSats }` for invoice. 400 invalid body, name, description, notes, time zone, status, period, or comment, self-donation, or `{ error: 'Expected a JSON body with an integer "amountSats"' }` when `amountSats` is missing, not a number, not a positive integer, or above 10_000_000. 401 missing bearer. 403 `{ error: 'SUNDAY_REST' }` for `comment`, `deleteComment`, and `invoice` on Sunday, or `{ error: 'Forbidden' }` for `deleteComment` below initiator rank. 404 missing habit or comment, including a comment id that is not a UUID on `deleteComment` and `invoice`. 409 `{ error: 'Period is closed' }` or `{ error: "The author's wallet cannot receive this Bitcoin payment", code: 'cannot_receive' }`. 429 `{ error: 'Too many payments' }`. 502 `{ error: 'Lightning Address could not be resolved' }` when the mint fails or the BOLT11 does not decode to exactly `amountSats * 1000` millisatoshis. 503 `{ error: 'Habits are unavailable' }` when the store throws (`habits.failed`).
- **Used by:** the habit tracker page.

## Endpoint: POST /me/wallet/report

- **Purpose:** The signed-in member's app reports its wallet after a sync: `{ balanceSats, syncedAt, payments }` with at most 200 payments per report (the first report pages through the full history). One `wallet_balance_snapshot` row is stored per report, and each valid payment is upserted into `wallet_payment` by `(account, id)`: status, amounts, method, and time are overwritten; category and counterparty are overwritten too, except that a later report whose category is only `outside_lightning`, `onchain`, or `unknown` keeps an already resolved category and its counterparty; detail fields (`paymentHash`, `invoice`, `destination`, `description`, `lnurlComment`) keep their stored value when the new report has none, `first_seen_at` (the observation time of the report that first stored the payment) never changes, `updated_at` moves only when a value changes, and a report observed before the latest accepted report of that payment does not overwrite it (every accepted report advances `last_observed_at`, also when it changes nothing). The category (`member`, `shop`, `platform`, `gift`, `outside_lightning`, `onchain`, `unknown`) and `counterpartyAccountId` come from `walletPaymentClassifier`, which uses forum and conversation invoices, indexed zap receipts, point-of-sale charges, own-host Lightning addresses, Spark identity keys, the platform account, and live shop notes. A payment needs an `id` of 1–256 characters without control characters that is not secret-shaped; a payment that fails this or another required rule is skipped, not stored, and not acknowledged. Unknown fields are ignored; a `preimage` is never read; secret-shaped detail values are stored as `null`.
- **Response:** 200 `{ acknowledgedIds }` — every stored payment id once. Invalid payments are skipped and not acknowledged, so the app sends them again.
- **Errors:** 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }`; 429 `{ error: 'Too many requests' }` after 60 reports per account in 60 seconds; 413 `{ error: 'Request body is too large' }` above 1 MiB; 400 `{ error: 'Invalid wallet report' }` for bad JSON, balance, sync time, or a `payments` value that is not an array of at most 200; 503 `{ error: 'Wallet data is unavailable' }` when a lookup or write fails (logs `wallet_report.write.failed` with `accountId` only).
- **Auth:** Bearer session. Not the debug token.

## Endpoint: POST /me/wallet/hearts

- **Purpose:** The signed-in member asks which forum post a heart payment was for. A heart is a 1-sat Spark payment whose invoice row has `heart` true. The Spark memo stays `zap:` plus 64 hex digits, the same memo as a living-room gift, so the wallet cannot tell them apart; this route is the lookup. It does not change gift classification, wallet reports, or invoice creation. The body is `{ hashes }` with 1 to 32 payment hashes (64 hex digits, either case). Each hash is looked up with `findOkInvoiceByPaymentHash` after lowercasing; duplicates collapse in first-seen order. A match is returned only when that invoice is a heart and the caller is the payer or the author. A gift that is not a heart, another member's heart, and a hash with no row are omitted, so the response does not reveal that a hash existed. `authorName` is the note's name trimmed, or empty when the note is missing; `text` is the note's text trimmed, or empty when the note is missing; both clip to 80 and 240 Unicode code points and append `…` when characters are dropped. `deleted` is true when the note is missing or `deletedAt` is set. A heart whose `messageId` is not a non-empty string is skipped. Lookups run one after another; a throw returns 503 with no partial list.
- **Response:** 200 `{ hearts: [{ paymentHash, messageId, authorName, text, deleted }] }` — `paymentHash` is lowercase 64 hex, in first-seen order after duplicates collapse. Omitted hashes are absent, not listed as errors.
- **Errors:** 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }`; 429 `{ error: 'Too many requests' }` after 60 lookups per account in 60 seconds; 413 `{ error: 'Request body is too large' }` above 8192 bytes; 400 `{ error: 'Invalid heart lookup' }` for bad JSON, a body that is not `{ hashes: string[] }`, an empty array, more than 32 hashes, or an element that is not 64 hex digits (the bad value is not echoed); 503 `{ error: 'Heart lookup is unavailable' }` when a lookup throws (logs `wallet_hearts.lookup.failed` with `accountId` only; no partial list).
- **Auth:** Bearer session. Not the debug token.

## Endpoint: POST /me/events

- **Purpose:** First-party interaction log of a signed-in member. The body `{ events: [{ name, at, path, props }] }` holds at most 50 events. `name` is one of the twenty-five names in `MEMBER_EVENT_NAMES` (`screen_view`, `post_created`, `reply_created`, `gift_sent`, `payment_sent`, `payment_failed`, `payment_received_seen`, `pos_charge_created`, `pos_charge_paid_seen`, `search`, `shop_opened`, `profile_opened`, `login`, `logout`, `signup_completed`, `wallet_open_failed`, `wallet_tour_shown`, `wallet_tour_step`, `wallet_tour_done`, `wallet_move_opened`, `wallet_move_step`, `wallet_move_copied`, `wallet_move_closed`, `wallet_move_done`, `recovery_done`, the last sent once after a social recovery sign-in); `at` is an ISO-8601 instant string (with `Z` or an offset) or epoch number within 2009-01-03 and now plus 5 minutes; `path` is cut at `?` or `#` and must start with `/` (at most 256 characters), and a path that holds a key token or a recovery-phrase run anywhere (as written and with runs of `%XX` escapes decoded as UTF-8, also across segments and inside encoded tokens) drops the whole event, counted in `dropped`; `props` is a flat object of at most 20 keys with `null`, boolean, finite number, or short string values. Secret-named keys and secret-shaped values are dropped. Valid events are stored in `member_event` with the account id and the receive time; invalid events are dropped and counted. Events before login are not collected; events are stored only in `member_event`.
- **Response:** 200 `{ accepted, dropped }`.
- **Errors:** 401 `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }`; 429 `{ error: 'Too many requests' }` after 30 batches per account in 60 seconds; 413 `{ error: 'Request body is too large' }` above 64 KiB; 400 `{ error: 'Invalid events' }` for bad JSON, a missing `events` array, or more than 50 events; 503 `{ error: 'Log is unavailable' }` when the insert fails.
- **Auth:** Bearer session. Not the debug token.

# Shop identity

## Endpoint: GET /shops

- **Purpose:** Public keyset page of public shops that already have a shop key. No session. A bearer is ignored and does not change the response. Each shop is `{ npub, name, place }` only: `npub` from the stored pubkey, `name` and `place` from `publicShop`. `place` is null when the note has no coordinates. `address` is null unless every structured part is non-blank. The ciphertext is not selected. `limit` defaults to 20 and is at most 100. `nextCursor` is present only when another structural row may exist. Order is the note's `createdAt` descending, then id descending. `/shops/activity` and `/shops/:npub` are unchanged.
- **Errors:** 400 `{ "error": "Invalid limit" }` or `{ "error": "Invalid cursor" }` with `Cache-Control: public, max-age=60`, without reading the store. A store throw is 503 `{ "error": "Shops are unavailable" }`, `Cache-Control: no-store`, and logs `shops.list.failed`.
- **Used by:** the app shop list via the same-origin proxy `/shops/list`, which this repo does not implement.
- **Auth:** none. Public. A bearer changes nothing.

## Endpoint: GET /shops/:npub

- **Purpose:** Resolve a shop identity without a session. A public identity returns its current name, description, place (null without coordinates; `address` null unless every structured part is non-blank), ordered photo URLs with header-only dimensions, till pay username, NIP-05, and creation time. A known lowercase hex pubkey redirects to the canonical npub URL; a retained identity that is no longer public returns 410 without its former name.
- **Inputs:** One path segment, validated as a lowercase 64-hex key or canonical lowercase npub.
- **Returns / side effects:** 200 `{ shop }`, 301 redirect, 404, 410, or 503. Public outcomes cache for 60 seconds; 503 is `no-store` and logs `shops.detail.failed`. While `nip05Domain` is set, a 200 reads the shared NIP-05 directory (`nip05Directory`: computed from every account, every shop identity and one bulk read of their notes, cached in process for 60 s per store set and shared with `/.well-known/nostr.json` and the shop kind 0, so a burst of requests computes it once). The entry must carry the name of the note this request read; on the first missing entry or other name the request asks once for a forced refresh (at most one computation per 10 s per store set; inside that gap the cached directory answers) and checks again; when it still does not match (renamed or no longer public between the two reads) it re-reads the note (a note no longer public is then 410), and a mismatch on the last round (`READ_ROUNDS`) is 503 with `shops.detail.failed` and `reason: 'nip05_stale'`. The photos come from one read of the whole set (`MessageStore.getPhotoSet`), never photo 0 of one set with the extras of another. Each URL carries the index the still is stored under (photo 0 `photo.<ext>`, extra `n` `photo/<n>.<ext>`), so a still with an unrecognized type drops only its own entry and an unrecognized photo 0 leaves the extras. After the photo set the note is read again; when its text, pin, till account id or `deletedAt` differ (a rename, text or pin change while the photos were read), the route goes round once more with that re-read, and a note that still differs on that last round is 503 with `shops.detail.failed` and `reason: 'note_changed'`, so a 200 never pairs the old name with newer photos. At most two rounds: three note reads, two photo set reads, one forced directory refresh.
- **Auth:** Public; bearer presence or validity does not change the response.
- **Success:** Returns a currently public shop, or redirects a known lowercase hex key to its npub URL.
- **Errors:** Unknown/invalid is 404, a known key whose note is not public is 410, and store failure, a NIP-05 entry that still does not carry the note's name after one re-read, or a note that still changes during the last photo read is 503 with no-store caching.
